===== FILE 2/58: .github/workflows/ci.yml (1641 bytes) =====
name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

env:
  CARGO_TERM_COLOR: always
  RUST_BACKTRACE: 1

jobs:
  fmt-check-clippy-test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:15-alpine
        env:
          POSTGRES_USER: medassist
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ci-not-a-secret
          POSTGRES_DB: medassist_test
        ports: ['5432:5432']
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U medassist"
          --health-interval 5s --health-timeout 5s --health-retries 10
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable
        with:
          components: rustfmt, clippy
      - uses: Swatinem/rust-cache@v2

      - name: Format check
        run: cargo fmt --all -- --check

      - name: Workspace check (all targets)
        run: cargo check --workspace --all-targets

      - name: Clippy (warnings are errors)
        run: cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings

      - name: Unit tests (no DB required)
        run: cargo test --workspace --lib

      - name: Apply migrations + integration tests (real PostgreSQL)
        env:
          DATABASE_URL: postgres://medassist:ci-not-a-secret@localhost:5432/medassist_test
        run: |
          cargo install sqlx-cli --no-default-features --features postgres --locked
          sqlx database setup
          cargo test --workspace -- --include-ignored

  license-audit:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: EmbarkStudios/cargo-deny-action@v2
        with:
          command: check licenses bans sources advisories

===== FILE 4/58: CHANGELOG.md (3290 bytes) =====
# CHANGELOG — MedAssist OSS

Project format: Keep a Changelog; this software follows Semantic Versioning.
All notable changes are documented here. Status labels distinguish what is
**implemented**, **compiled**, **tested** and **validated in execution**.

## [Unreleased] — development snapshot (2026-10-16)

### Added
- Cargo workspace with 5 crates: `medassist-domain`, `medassist-application`,
  `medassist-infrastructure`, `medassist-api`, `medassist-cli`.
- **Domain crate** (compiled + 36/36 unit tests passing): Patient (stable UUID
  identity, external identifiers, never name-only matching), Encounter, SOAP
  Note model with draft/review/finalize/amend state machine, version chain with
  optimistic-concurrency conflict detection, Provenance tagging (human vs AI vs
  imported), Medication, Allergy, VitalSigns, Transcription (segments +
  timestamps + consent), Job state machine, Consent record, Identifier types.
- **Application crate** (compiled + 21/21 unit tests passing incl. cross-org
  isolation for documents): ports (traits) for repositories, auth, AI engines;
  use-cases for patients, encounters, notes (save/draft/finalize with human
  edits never silently overwritten by async AI), auth service (Argon2id, lockout
  after failed attempts, opaque expiring session tokens, revocation), audit
  service, transcription orchestration, document import with size/type
  validation and SHA-256 hashing, template registry, AI anti-hallucination
  guardrails (unknown fields become flagged gaps, never invented facts).
- **Infrastructure crate** (compiles): SQLx/PostgreSQL repositories (patients,
  notes, documents, jobs, users, sessions, audit), filesystem blob store with
  path sanitization + immutable writes, deterministic test-double AI engine
  (clearly labeled as a double, not a real model), Ollama HTTP adapter skeleton
  with timeouts/cancellation.
- PostgreSQL migration `migrations/20261017000001_schema.sql`: multi-tenant
  schema with `org_id` on every clinical table, FK constraints, justified
  indexes, UTC timestamptz columns.
- Axum API skeleton: typed error envelope (`ApiError`), health/readiness
  endpoints, request tracing middleware, state wiring.
- CLI crate skeleton.
- Licensing & governance: full GPL-3.0 text (`LICENSE`), `README.md`,
  `NOTICE.md`, `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md`, `SECURITY.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`,
  `.env.example`, `CHANGELOG.md`.
- CI: GitHub Actions workflow (fmt/check/clippy -D warnings/tests with real
  Postgres service) + `cargo-deny` config (`deny.toml`).

### Fixed
- Workspace manifest errors (missing api/cli crate manifests, sqlx feature
  misconfiguration) that prevented compilation.

### Known limitations / not yet done
- API routes beyond health checks return 501-style typed "not implemented"
  errors; CRUD endpoints over the application services are pending.
- Integration tests against live PostgreSQL written but gated on DATABASE_URL;
  executed locally in dev environment only at time of writing.
- Leptos web UI, Tauri desktop app, FHIR mapping, RAG retrieval, OCR adapters,
  CID-10 tables, audio capture frontend: not started.
- No third-party security audit performed. Clinical safety validation not
  performed — this tool supports documentation and does not replace
  professional judgment.

===== FILE 5/58: CONTRIBUTING.md (2664 bytes) =====
# Contributing to MedAssist OSS

Thank you for your interest in contributing. This project is licensed under
**GNU GPL v3.0 or later**; contributions are accepted under the same terms.

## Licensing conditions (inbound = outbound)

1. Every contribution you submit must be your own original work, or properly
   attributed material that is compatible with GPL-3.0-or-later.
2. By opening a pull request you automatically license your contribution under
   GPL-3.0-or-later. No separate CLA signature step exists yet; if one is added
   later, it will be documented here and PRs will state it explicitly.
3. Do not introduce dependencies whose licenses are incompatible with GPL-3.0
   (e.g., GPL-1.0/2.0-only without "or later", proprietary SDKs). Check
   `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md` and CI (`cargo-deny`) before adding crates.
4. Model weights, datasets and clinical terminology sources have their own
   licenses — never commit large model weights or restricted data without an
   explicit redistribution grant recorded in `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md`.

## Development workflow

```bash
rustup show                      # toolchain pinned in rust-toolchain.toml
cp .env.example .env             # fill local values; never commit .env
sqlx database setup              # applies migrations from ./migrations
cargo run -p medassist-cli       # or cargo run -p medassist-api
```

Before opening a PR, all of these must pass:

```bash
cargo fmt --all -- --check
cargo check --workspace --all-targets
cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings
cargo test --workspace
```

Integration tests requiring PostgreSQL are gated behind the `DATABASE_URL`
environment variable and are skipped when it is absent; CI provisions a real
database so they actually run there.

## Code and clinical-safety rules

- Rust is the primary language; keep domain logic free of Axum/SQLx/UI types.
- Never mark an AI-generated note as final without human review paths being
  exercised by tests.
- New endpoints require authorization checks tested against cross-organization
  access attempts.
- Use only synthetic/pseudonymous data in tests, fixtures and docs.
- Log statements must not emit credentials, tokens or full clinical content.

## Reporting issues

- Security vulnerabilities: see `SECURITY.md`, do not open public issues.
- Bugs: include exact commands, Rust version (`rustc --version`), OS, and
  failure output. Redact any real patient data.

## Style

- `cargo fmt` output is authoritative; clippy warnings are errors.
- Public items need doc comments explaining *why*, especially safety-relevant
  invariants (provenance, draft vs. finalized state, org isolation).

stand ready to extend this provision to those domains in future versions
of the GPL, as needed to protect the freedom of users.

  Finally, every program is threatened constantly by software patents.
States should not allow patents to restrict development and use of

   `argon2` crate (RustCrypto), MIT/Apache-2.0. We use Argon2id parameters as

   is a registered trademark of the PostgreSQL Global Development Group.

   project. All implementations are original work under GPL-3.0-or-later.

   Mistral models) carry their own licenses and notices which must accompany
   any redistribution of derived artifacts. See `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md`.

===== FILE 10/58: README.md (5915 bytes) =====
# MedAssist OSS

**Open-source, self-hostable, AI-assisted clinical documentation.**
Rust-first backend (Axum + SQLx + PostgreSQL), privacy-by-design, human-in-the-loop
for every AI suggestion. Licensed **GPL-3.0-or-later**.

> ⚠️ Clinical-use disclaimer: MedAssist OSS is a documentation *support* tool.
> It does not diagnose, does not prescribe, and does not replace professional
> judgment. AI output is always an unreviewed draft until a qualified human
> finalizes it. No clinical-safety validation has been performed.

## Status (honest labels)

| Area | Implemented | Compiled | Unit-tested | Integration/execution-validated |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Domain model (patient, encounter, SOAP note, versions, provenance, meds, allergies, vitals, jobs, consent) | OK | OK | 36/36 passing | pending live DB run |
| Application services (auth Argon2id+lockout, sessions, notes w/ conflict detection, docs w/ hash+limits, audit, AI guardrails) | OK | OK | 21/21 passing | pending live DB run |
| PostgreSQL schema + migrations | OK | OK | -- | gated on `DATABASE_URL` |
| SQLx repositories / blob store / AI test-double / Ollama adapter skeleton | OK | OK | partial | pending live DB run |
| Axum API (health/readiness, typed errors, tracing) | OK | OK | OK | running binary |
| CRUD/clinical endpoints over services | NOT YET (stubs) | OK | -- | -- |
| Web UI (Leptos), desktop (Tauri), FHIR, RAG, OCR, audio capture | NOT STARTED | -- | -- | -- |

## Features

- **Patients**: stable UUID identity, external identifiers, duplicate-control
  that never merges on name alone; search with pagination.
- **Encounters & SOAP notes**: draft -> review -> finalize -> amend state
  machine; full version history; optimistic-concurrency edit-conflict detection.
- **Provenance everywhere**: every field tagged human / AI-draft / imported,
  with source references. AI suggestions can never silently overwrite human
  edits and cannot finalize notes autonomously.
- **AI engine abstraction** (`ClinicalDocumentationEngine`,
  `LanguageModelProvider`, `TranscriptionEngine`): local-first (Ollama adapter),
  deterministic test double for CI; anti-hallucination guardrails turn missing
  information into explicit flagged gaps instead of invented facts.
- **Documents/labs**: upload validation (size/type), SHA-256 integrity hashing,
  immutable originals, extracted values linked back to their source document.
- **Multi-tenant isolation** by `org_id` enforced server-side; cross-org access
  attempts are covered by tests.
- **Audit trail**: actor/action/resource/timestamp/outcome without embedding
  full clinical content.
- **Auth**: Argon2id password hashes, failed-attempt lockout, opaque expiring
  revocable session tokens.

## Requirements

- Rust stable (pinned in `rust-toolchain.toml`)
- PostgreSQL >= 14
- Linux/macOS (Windows via WSL supported for development)

## Quick start (development)

```bash
git clone <repo-url> && cd medassist-oss
cp .env.example .env          # edit DATABASE_URL etc.; never commit .env

# Start PostgreSQL (e.g., docker/podman):
docker run -d --name medassist-pg -e POSTGRES_USER=medassist \
  -e POSTGRES_PASSWORD=<choose-a-secret> -e POSTGRES_DB=medassist \
  -p 127.0.0.1:5432:5432 postgres:15-alpine

cargo run -p medassist-cli -- migrate   # applies ./migrations
cargo run -p medassist-api              # http://127.0.0.1:8080
curl http://127.0.0.1:8080/healthz      # liveness
curl http://127.0.0.1:8080/readyz       # readiness incl. DB check
```

### First administrator

There is **no default admin account or universal password**. Bootstrap the
first admin via the CLI once the `create-admin` command is wired; until then
use the documented SQL bootstrap in `docs/deployment.md`. Never ship secrets
in code or logs.

## Tests

```bash
cargo test --workspace                 # unit tests, no DB needed
DATABASE_URL=postgres://... cargo test --workspace -- --include-ignored   # integration
cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings
cargo fmt --all -- --check
cargo deny check licenses              # license allow-list audit
```

## Configuration

All configuration is environment-driven (see `.env.example`):
`DATABASE_URL`, `MEDASSIST_BIND`, `MEDASSIST_ENV` (`development|production`),
`MEDASSIST_BLOB_DIR`. Production refuses to start with development defaults.
AI providers are opt-in: nothing leaves the machine unless an operator
explicitly configures a remote endpoint.

## Limitations (current release)

- Only health endpoints are functional in the HTTP API; clinical REST routes
  are next on the roadmap (services already exist behind them).
- Transcription/AI real-model execution requires an operator-provided local
  provider (e.g., Ollama); none is bundled. No weights ship with the repo.
- Accessibility (WCAG 2.2 AA) work begins with the web UI.
- Not production-hardened: no pen-test or regulatory certification performed.

===== FILE 11/58: SECURITY.md (2374 bytes) =====
# Security Policy — MedAssist OSS

## Reporting a vulnerability

MedAssist OSS handles sensitive clinical data. If you believe you found a
security issue, please report it responsibly.

**Current limitation (honest disclosure):** this project does not yet operate a
private issue tracker or a verified security contact address. Until one is
established in the public repository, report vulnerabilities through the
repository's private security advisory mechanism (GitHub: *Security → Report a
vulnerability*) if available, or by opening an issue **without** disclosing
exploitable details publicly until a fix is released.

Do **not** post full exploit code, real patient data, or reproduction steps
containing protected health information in public channels.

## Scope and design commitments

- Clinical information is treated as sensitive personal data (LGPD art. 11).
- Passwords are hashed with Argon2id; plaintext passwords are never stored.
- Session tokens are opaque, expiring, and revocable; cookies use
  `HttpOnly`, `Secure`, `SameSite=Strict` in production.
- All SQL goes through parameterized SQLx queries; no string interpolation.
- Multi-tenant isolation is enforced server-side via `org_id`; a client-supplied
  identifier is never treated as proof of authorization.
- Audit events record actor, action, resource, timestamp and outcome — but not
  full clinical content by default.
- AI output is always a *draft* requiring human review before finalization;
  prompts, transcriptions and model responses are treated as untrusted input
  (prompt-injection defense).
- No clinical data is sent to external providers unless explicitly configured
  and authorized by the operator.
- Logs exclude credentials, tokens and full transcriptions by default.

## What is NOT yet implemented (as of this writing)

- TLS termination configuration examples are documented but not enforced by the
  application itself (use a reverse proxy or enable Postgres/TLS at deploy time).
- Automated penetration testing and third-party security audit have not been
  performed. Do not assume the software is certified secure for production use.

## Data protection notes

- Retention policies for audio, transcripts, documents and backups must be
  configured per organization; see `docs/privacy.md`.
- Never commit real `.env` files; `.env.example` ships placeholders only.

===== FILE 14/58: crates/medassist-api/src/error.rs (2878 bytes) =====
//! Uniform JSON error envelope. Never leaks internal causes to clients.

use axum::{
    http::StatusCode,
    response::{IntoResponse, Response},
    Json,
};
use medassist_application::AppError;
use serde_json::json;

/// Wrapper so handlers can return `Result<T, ApiError>` directly.
pub struct ApiError(pub AppError);

impl From<AppError> for ApiError {
    fn from(e: AppError) -> Self {
        Self(e)
    }
}

impl ApiError {
    pub fn bad_request(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self(AppError::Unprocessable(msg.into()))
    }
    pub fn unauthorized() -> Self {
        Self(AppError::Unauthorized)
    }
}

impl IntoResponse for ApiError {
    fn into_response(self) -> Response {
        let (status, code, message) = match &self.0 {
            AppError::Domain(de) => (domain_status(de), self.0.code(), de.to_string()),
            AppError::Unauthenticated => (StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED, "unauthenticated", "invalid credentials".into()),
            AppError::Unauthorized => (StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED, "unauthenticated", "authentication required".into()),
            AppError::Forbidden(m) => (StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, "forbidden", m.to_string()),
            AppError::NotFound(_) => (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "not_found", "resource not found".into()),
            AppError::Conflict(m) => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "conflict", m.clone()),
            AppError::Unprocessable(m) => (StatusCode::UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY, "unprocessable", m.clone()),
            AppError::ExternalService(_) => (
                StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,
                "external_service_error",
                "external AI service error".into(),
            ),
            AppError::Timeout => (StatusCode::GATEWAY_TIMEOUT, "timeout", "operation timed out".into()),
            AppError::Cancelled => (StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, "cancelled", "operation cancelled".into()),
            AppError::Internal(_) => {
                // Log the fact, never the cause.
                tracing::error!("internal server error (cause hidden)");
                (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, "internal_error", "internal error".into())
            }
        };
        // Message sanitisation: domain/app messages are written by us and are
        // safe (no PHI, no driver details). Internal errors are masked above.
        (status, Json(json!({ "error": { "code": code, "message": message } }))).into_response()
    }
}

fn domain_status(e: &medassist_domain::DomainError) -> StatusCode {
    use medassist_domain::DomainError::*;
    match e {
        Validation { .. } | InvalidDateRange { .. } => StatusCode::UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
        NotFound(_) => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
        VersionConflict { .. } | IllegalTransition { .. } | DuplicateCandidate(_) => StatusCode::CONFLICT,
        ImmutableField | ProvenanceRequired(_) => StatusCode::UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
    }
}

===== FILE 15/58: crates/medassist-api/src/lib.rs (1008 bytes) =====
//! Axum HTTP API for MedAssist OSS. Transport only: request parsing, auth
//! extraction, error mapping. All behaviour lives in the application layer.
//!
//! License: GPL-3.0-or-later.

pub mod error;
pub mod routes;
pub mod state;

use axum::routing::get;
use tower_http::limit::RequestBodyLimitLayer;
use tower_http::trace::TraceLayer;

use state::AppState;

pub const MAX_BODY_BYTES: usize = 26 * 1024 * 1024; // 25 MiB docs + headroom

/// Build the router. `ui` optionally serves the static web app at `/`.
pub fn build_router(state: AppState, ui: Option<axum::Router>) -> axum::Router {
    let api = axum::Router::new()
        .route("/healthz", get(routes::health_live))
        .route("/readyz", get(routes::health_ready))
        .nest("/api/v1", routes::api_v1(state.clone()))
        .layer(RequestBodyLimitLayer::new(MAX_BODY_BYTES))
        .with_state(state);

    let mut app = api;
    if let Some(ui) = ui {
        app = app.merge(ui);
    }
    app.layer(TraceLayer::new_for_http())
}

===== FILE 16/58: crates/medassist-api/src/routes.rs (36019 bytes) =====
//! Route table + handlers for the versioned REST API. Thin transport over the
//! application services; every handler enforces authentication via extractor.


use axum::{
    extract::{Multipart, Path, Query, Request, State},
    middleware::{self, Next},
    response::{IntoResponse, Response},
    Json, Router,
};
use axum::routing::{get, post};
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

use medassist_application::{
    ai::DocumentationRequest,
    clamp_page,
    errors::Actor,
    ports::PatientQuery as RepoPatientQuery,
};
use medassist_domain::identifier::ExternalIdentifier;
use medassist_domain::note::NoteBody;
use medassist_domain::patient::{NewPatient, Sex};
use medassist_domain::encounter::EncounterStatus;

use crate::error::ApiError;
use crate::state::{AppState, EngineChoice};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Authentication extractor
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Authenticated actor attached to requests by `require_auth`.
#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct Authed(pub Actor);

impl axum::extract::FromRequestParts<AppState> for Authed {
    type Rejection = ApiError;
    async fn from_request_parts(
        parts: &mut axum::http::request::Parts,
        state: &AppState,
    ) -> Result<Self, Self::Rejection> {
        let _ = state;
        parts
            .extensions
            .get::<Authed>()
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(ApiError::unauthorized)
    }
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn require_auth(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    next: Next,
    req: Request,
) -> Response {
    {
        let token = extract_token(&req).to_string();
        if token.is_empty() {
            return ApiError::unauthorized().into_response();
        }
        match st.auth.resolve_session(&token, Utc::now()).await {
            Ok(actor) => {
                let mut req = req;
                req.extensions_mut().insert(Authed(actor));
                next.run(req).await
            }
            Err(e) => ApiError::from(e).into_response(),
        }
    }
}

fn extract_token(req: &Request) -> &str {
    if let Some(h) = req.headers().get(axum::http::header::AUTHORIZATION) {
        if let Some(rest) = h.to_str().ok().and_then(|s| s.strip_prefix("Bearer ")) {
            return rest.trim();
        }
    }
    if let Some(cookie) = req.headers().get(axum::http::header::COOKIE) {
        if let Ok(s) = cookie.to_str() {
            for part in s.split(';') {
                let part = part.trim();
                if let Some(v) = part.strip_prefix("medassist_session=") {
                    return v.trim();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    ""
}

fn actor_of(req: &Request) -> Result<Actor, ApiError> {
    req.extensions()
        .get::<Authed>()
        .map(|a| a.0.clone())
        .ok_or_else(ApiError::unauthorized)
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Router
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

pub fn api_v1(st: AppState) -> Router<AppState> {
    let authed = Router::new()
        // patients
        .route("/patients", get(list_patients).post(create_patient))
        .route("/patients/:id", get(get_patient).patch(update_patient).delete(delete_patient))
        .route("/patients/:id/encounters", get(list_encounters).post(create_encounter))
        // encounters
        .route("/encounters/:id", get(get_encounter).patch(patch_encounter))
        .route("/encounters/:id/transition", axum::routing::post(transition_encounter))
        .route("/encounters/:id/notes", get(list_notes).post(create_note))
        // notes
        .route("/notes/:id", get(get_note).patch(edit_note))
        .route("/notes/:id/versions", get(list_versions))
        .route("/notes/:id/review-section", axum::routing::post(review_section))
        .route("/notes/:id/finalize", axum::routing::post(finalize_note))
        .route("/notes/:id/amend", axum::routing::post(amend_note))
        // documents
        .route("/documents", axum::routing::post(upload_document))
        .route("/documents/:id", get(get_document))
        .route("/documents/:id/content", get(download_document))
        .route("/documents/:id/review", axum::routing::post(review_document))
        .route("/patients/:id/documents", get(list_patient_documents))
        // transcription
        .route("/consents", axum::routing::post(create_consent))
        .route("/transcriptions", axum::routing::post(start_transcription))
        .route("/transcriptions/:id", get(get_transcription))
        .route("/transcriptions/:id/cancel", axum::routing::post(cancel_transcription))
        .route("/transcriptions/:id/review", axum::routing::post(review_transcription))
        .route("/transcriptions/:id/draft-body", get(transcription_draft_body))
        // AI drafts (async jobs)
        .route("/ai/documentation-drafts", axum::routing::post(create_ai_draft))
        .route("/jobs/:id", get(get_job))
        // templates / coding / audit / capabilities
        .route("/templates", get(list_templates))
        .route("/codes/search", get(search_codes))
        .route("/audit-events", get(list_audit))
        .route("/system/capabilities", get(capabilities))
        .route("/auth/logout", axum::routing::post(logout))
        .layer(middleware::from_fn_with_state(st.clone(), require_auth));

    let public = Router::new().route("/auth/login", post({
        let st = st.clone();
        move |Json(r): Json<LoginReq>| login(st.clone(), Json(r))
    }));

    public.merge(authed).with_state(st)
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Health
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

pub async fn health_live() -> impl IntoResponse {
    (
        axum::http::StatusCode::OK,
        Json(serde_json::json!({ "status": "ok", "version": env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION") })),
    )
}

pub async fn health_ready(State(st): State<AppState>) -> Response {
    use sqlx::Row;
    match sqlx::query("SELECT 1 AS ok").fetch_one(&st.jobs.0).await {
        Ok(r) if r.try_get::<i32, _>("ok").unwrap_or(0) == 1 => {
            (axum::http::StatusCode::OK, Json(serde_json::json!({ "database": "up" }))).into_response()
        }
        _ => (
            axum::http::StatusCode::SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            Json(serde_json::json!({ "database": "down" })),
        )
            .into_response(),
    }
}

    encounter_id: Uuid,
    soap: SoapDto,
    template_id: Option<Uuid>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct EditNoteDto {
    expected_version: u32,
    soap: SoapDto,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ReviewSectionDto {
    section: String,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct FinalizeDto {
    #[serde(default)]
    override_gaps: bool,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct AmendDto {
    soap: SoapDto,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ConsentDto {
    patient_id: Uuid,
    granted_by: String,
    scope: String,
    retention_policy: String,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct StartTranscriptionDto {
    patient_id: Uuid,
    encounter_id: Option<Uuid>,
    consent_id: Uuid,
    engine: Option<String>,
    retention_policy: String,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct AiDraftDto {
    encounter_id: Uuid,
    sections: Option<Vec<String>>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct CodeSearchDto {
    system: Option<String>,
    text: String,
    limit: Option<i64>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct AuditQuery {
    limit: Option<i64>,
    offset: Option<i64>,
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn soap_to_body(dto: SoapDto, author: Uuid) -> NoteBody {
    use medassist_domain::note::{SoapContent, SoapSection};
    NoteBody::Soap(SoapContent {
        subjective: dto.subjective.filter(|t| !t.trim().is_empty()).map(|t| SoapSection::professional(author, t)),
        objective: dto.objective.filter(|t| !t.trim().is_empty()).map(|t| SoapSection::professional(author, t)),
        assessment: dto.assessment.filter(|t| !t.trim().is_empty()).map(|t| SoapSection::professional(author, t)),
        plan: dto.plan.filter(|t| !t.trim().is_empty()).map(|t| SoapSection::professional(author, t)),
    })
}

fn body_to_soap(body: &NoteBody) -> serde_json::Value {
    use medassist_domain::note::NoteBody::*;
    match body {
        Soap(s) => serde_json::json!({
            "kind": "soap",
            "subjective": s.subjective,
            "objective": s.objective,
            "assessment": s.assessment,
            "plan": s.plan,
            "missing_sections": s.missing_sections(),
            "unreviewed_sections": s.unreviewed_sections(),
        }),
        FreeText { text, provenance, reviewed } => serde_json::json!({
            "kind": "free_text", "text": text, "provenance": provenance, "reviewed": reviewed
        }),
    }
}

fn now() -> DateTime<Utc> {
    Utc::now()
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Auth handlers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn login(
    st: AppState,
    Json(req): Json<LoginReq>,
) -> std::pin::Pin<Box<dyn std::future::Future<Output = Result<Response, ApiError>> + Send>> {
    Box::pin(async move {
    let (token, actor) = st.auth.login(&req.email, &req.password, now()).await?;
    let cookie = format!(
        "medassist_session={token}; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/; Max-Age=28800"
    );
    let resp = Json(LoginResp {
        session_token: token.clone(),
        user_id: actor.user_id,
        org_id: actor.org_id,
        roles: actor.roles.clone(),
    });
        Ok((
            [(axum::http::header::SET_COOKIE, cookie)],
            resp,
        )
            .into_response())
    })
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn logout(State(st): State<AppState>, req: Request) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {
    let token = extract_token(&req).to_string();
    st.auth.logout(&token, now()).await.map_err(ApiError::from)?;
    Ok(Json(serde_json::json!({ "ok": true })))
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Patients
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn create_patient(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    Json(dto): Json<NewPatientDto>,

    Authed(actor): Authed,) -> Result<(axum::http::StatusCode, Json<serde_json::Value>), ApiError> {
    let sex = match dto.sex.as_deref() {
        None => None,
        Some(s) => Some(match s {
            "female" => Sex::Female,
            "male" => Sex::Male,
            "intersex" => Sex::Intersex,
            "unknown" => Sex::Unknown,
            "undisclosed" => Sex::Undisclosed,
            _ => return Err(ApiError::bad_request(format!("invalid sex '{s}'"))),
        }),
    };
    let external_ids = dto
        .external_ids
        .into_iter()
        .map(|e| {
            let system = match e.system.as_str() {
                "cns" => medassist_domain::identifier::ExternalIdSystem::Cns,
                "mrn" => medassist_domain::identifier::ExternalIdSystem::Mrn,
                "health_plan" => medassist_domain::identifier::ExternalIdSystem::HealthPlan,
                "passport" => medassist_domain::identifier::ExternalIdSystem::Passport,
                _ => medassist_domain::identifier::ExternalIdSystem::Other,
            };
            ExternalIdentifier { system, value: e.value, issuer: e.issuer }
        })
        .collect();
    let input = NewPatient {
        org_id: actor.org_id,
        family_name: dto.family_name,
        given_name: dto.given_name,
        birth_date: dto.birth_date,
        sex,
        contact: dto.contact,
        external_ids,
        notes: dto.notes,
    };
    let p = st.patients.create(&actor, input, dto.allow_duplicates, now()).await?;
    Ok((axum::http::StatusCode::CREATED, Json(serde_json::to_value(&p).unwrap())))
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn list_patients(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    req: Request,
    Query(q): Query<SearchQuery>,
) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {
    let actor = actor_of(&req)?;
    let (limit, offset) = clamp_page(q.limit, q.offset);
    let page = st
        .patients
        .search(
            &actor,
            RepoPatientQuery { text: q.q, limit, offset },
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(Json(serde_json::json!({
        "items": page.items, "total": page.total, "limit": page.limit, "offset": page.offset
    })))
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn get_patient(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    req: Request,
    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {
    let actor = actor_of(&req)?;
    let p = st.patients.get(&actor, id).await?;
    Ok(Json(serde_json::to_value(&p).unwrap()))
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn update_patient(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(dto): Json<UpdatePatientDto>,

    Authed(actor): Authed,) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {
    let parse_sex = |s: &Option<Option<String>>| -> Result<Option<Option<Sex>>, ApiError> {
        match s {
            None => Ok(None),
            Some(inner) => match inner.as_deref() {
                None => Ok(Some(None)),
                Some(v) => Ok(Some(Some(match v {
                    "female" => Sex::Female,
                    "male" => Sex::Male,
                    "intersex" => Sex::Intersex,
                    "unknown" => Sex::Unknown,
                    "undisclosed" => Sex::Undisclosed,
                    _ => return Err(ApiError::bad_request(format!("invalid sex '{v}'"))),
                }))),
            },
        }
    };
    let p = st
        .patients
        .update(
            &actor,
            id,
            dto.family_name,
            dto.given_name,
            dto.birth_date,
            parse_sex(&dto.sex)?,
            dto.contact,
            dto.notes,
            now(),
        )
        .await?;
    Ok(Json(serde_json::to_value(&p).unwrap()))
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn delete_patient(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    req: Request,
    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {
    let actor = actor_of(&req)?;
    st.patients.soft_delete(&actor, id, now()).await?;
    Ok(Json(serde_json::json!({ "ok": true, "deleted": id })))
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn create_encounter(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    Path(patient_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(dto): Json<NewEncounterDto>,

    Authed(actor): Authed,) -> Result<(axum::http::StatusCode, Json<serde_json::Value>), ApiError> {
    if dto.patient_id != patient_id {
        return Err(ApiError::bad_request("path/query patient_id mismatch"));
    }
    let e = st
        .encounters
        .create(
            &actor,
            medassist_domain::encounter::NewEncounter {
                org_id: actor.org_id,
                patient_id,
                practitioner_id: actor.user_id,
                chief_complaint: dto.chief_complaint,
            },
            now(),
        )
        .await?;
    Ok((axum::http::StatusCode::CREATED, Json(serde_json::to_value(&e).unwrap())))
}

) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {

        "items": page.items, "total": page.total, "limit": page.limit, "offset": page.offset
    })))
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn patch_encounter(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(dto): Json<PatchEncounterDto>,

    Authed(actor): Authed,) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {
    let cc = dto.chief_complaint.ok_or_else(|| ApiError::bad_request("chief_complaint required"))?;

    Ok(Json(serde_json::to_value(&e).unwrap()))

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn transition_encounter(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(dto): Json<TransitionDto>,

    Authed(actor): Authed,) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {
    let to = match dto.to.as_str() {
        "open" => EncounterStatus::Open,
        "in_progress" => EncounterStatus::InProgress,
        "closed" => EncounterStatus::Closed,
        "finalised" => EncounterStatus::Finalised,
        "cancelled" => EncounterStatus::Cancelled,
        s => return Err(ApiError::bad_request(format!("invalid status '{s}'"))),
    };
    let e = st.encounters.transition(&actor, id, to, now()).await?;
    Ok(Json(serde_json::to_value(&e).unwrap()))
}

}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn list_versions(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    req: Request,
    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {
    let actor = actor_of(&req)?;
    // Read directly from the version store through the note repo port is not
    // exposed; use SQL here — versions are immutable snapshots.
    let rows = sqlx::query(
        "SELECT nv.version, nv.edited_by, nv.created_at, nv.body
         FROM note_versions nv JOIN notes n ON n.id = nv.note_id
         WHERE nv.note_id = $1 AND n.org_id = $2
         ORDER BY nv.version ASC",
    )
    .bind(id)
    .bind(actor.org_id)
    .fetch_all(&st.jobs.0)
    .await
    .map_err(|e| ApiError::from(medassist_application::AppError::storage(e)))?;
    use sqlx::Row;
    let items: Vec<_> = rows
        .iter()
        .map(|r| {
            serde_json::json!({
                "version": r.get::<i32, _>("version"),
                "edited_by": r.get::<Uuid, _>("edited_by"),
                "created_at": r.get::<DateTime<Utc>, _>("created_at").to_rfc3339(),
                "body": r.get::<serde_json::Value, _>("body"),
            })
        })
        .collect();
    Ok(Json(serde_json::json!({ "note_id": id, "versions": items })))
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Documents
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn upload_document(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    mut mp: Multipart,
    Authed(actor): Authed,
) -> Result<(axum::http::StatusCode, Json<serde_json::Value>), ApiError> {

    let mut patient_id: Option<Uuid> = None;
    let mut encounter_id: Option<Uuid> = None;
    let mut filename = String::new();
    let mut field_ct = String::new();
    let mut bytes: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();

    while let Some(field) = mp.next_field().await.map_err(|e| ApiError::bad_request(format!("multipart error: {e}")))? {
        let name = field.name().unwrap_or("").to_string();
        match name.as_str() {
            "patient_id" => {
                let t = field.text().await.map_err(|_| ApiError::bad_request("bad patient_id"))?;
                patient_id = Some(Uuid::parse_str(&t).map_err(|_| ApiError::bad_request("patient_id must be uuid"))?);
            }
            "encounter_id" => {
                let t = field.text().await.map_err(|_| ApiError::bad_request("bad encounter_id"))?;
                if !t.trim().is_empty() {
                    encounter_id = Some(Uuid::parse_str(t.trim()).map_err(|_| ApiError::bad_request("encounter_id must be uuid"))?);
                }
            }
            "file" => {
                filename = field.file_name().unwrap_or("document").to_string();
                field_ct = field.content_type().map(|m| m.to_string()).unwrap_or_else(|| "application/octet-stream".to_string());
                bytes = field.bytes().await.map_err(|_| ApiError::bad_request("failed reading file"))?.to_vec();
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    let patient_id = patient_id.ok_or_else(|| ApiError::bad_request("patient_id field required"))?;
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return Err(ApiError::bad_request("file field required and non-empty"));
    }
    let doc = st
        .documents
        .import(&actor, patient_id, encounter_id, filename, field_ct, bytes)
        .await?;
    Ok((axum::http::StatusCode::CREATED, Json(serde_json::to_value(&doc).unwrap())))
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn get_document(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    req: Request,
    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {
    let actor = actor_of(&req)?;
    let d = st.documents.get(&actor, id).await?;
    Ok(Json(serde_json::to_value(&d).unwrap()))
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn download_document(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    req: Request,
    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Response, ApiError> {
    let actor = actor_of(&req)?;
    let d = st.documents.get(&actor, id).await?;
    let bytes = st.documents.download(&actor, id).await?;
    Ok((
        [
            (axum::http::header::CONTENT_TYPE, d.content_type.clone()),
            (
                axum::http::header::CONTENT_DISPOSITION,
                format!("attachment; filename=\"{}\"", d.filename.replace('"', "")),
            ),
        ],
        bytes,
    )
        .into_response())
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn review_document(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    req: Request,
    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {
    let actor = actor_of(&req)?;
    st.documents.mark_reviewed(&actor, id).await?;
    Ok(Json(serde_json::json!({ "ok": true, "state": "reviewed" })))
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// AI drafts + jobs
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn create_ai_draft(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    Json(dto): Json<AiDraftDto>,

    Authed(actor): Authed,) -> Result<(axum::http::StatusCode, Json<serde_json::Value>), ApiError> {
    use medassist_application::ports::JobRepository;
    let enc = st.encounters.get(&actor, dto.encounter_id).await?;
    let job = medassist_domain::job::AsyncJob::new(Uuid::new_v4(), actor.org_id, medassist_domain::job::JobKind::AiDocumentationDraft, now());
    st.jobs.insert(&job).await?;
    let request = DocumentationRequest {
        job_id: job.id,
        org_id: actor.org_id,
        patient_id: enc.patient_id,
        encounter_id: enc.id,
        sections: dto.sections.unwrap_or_else(|| vec!["subjective".into(), "objective".into(), "assessment".into(), "plan".into()]),
        transcript: None,
        authorised_context: enc.chief_complaint.clone().map(|c| vec![format!("queixa: {c}")]).unwrap_or_default(),
    };
    let note = st
        .notes
        .create_draft(
            &actor,
            enc.id,
            NoteBody::Soap(medassist_domain::note::SoapContent::default()),
            None,
            now(),
        )
        .await?;
    // Persist the job->note link and enqueue work for the background worker.
    let payload = serde_json::json!({ "request": request, "note_id": note.id, "actor_user_id": actor.user_id });
    sqlx::query("UPDATE jobs SET result_ref=$2 WHERE id=$1")
        .bind(job.id)
        .bind(payload.to_string())
        .execute(&st.jobs.0)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| ApiError::from(medassist_application::AppError::storage(e)))?;
    st.sink
        .record(medassist_application::ports::AuditEvent {
            time: now(),
            actor_user_id: Some(actor.user_id),
            org_id: Some(actor.org_id),
            action: "ai.draft.requested".into(),
            resource_type: "job".into(),
            resource_id: Some(job.id),
            outcome: medassist_application::ports::AuditOutcome::Success,
            metadata: serde_json::json!({ "engine": st.llm_id, "external": st.llm_external }),
        })
        .await;
    Ok((
        axum::http::StatusCode::ACCEPTED,
        Json(serde_json::json!({ "job_id": job.id, "note_id": note.id, "state": job.state.as_str() })),
    ))
}

#[axum::debug_handler]
async fn get_job(
    State(st): State<AppState>,
    req: Request,
    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<serde_json::Value>, ApiError> {
    use medassist_application::ports::JobRepository;
    let actor = actor_of(&req)?;
    let j = st
        .jobs
        .get(&actor, id)
        .await?
        .ok_or(medassist_application::AppError::NotFound("job"))?;
    Ok(Json(serde_json::json!({
        "id": j.id, "kind": j.kind, "state": j.state.as_str(),
        "attempts": j.attempts, "max_attempts": j.max_attempts,
        "error": j.error, "result_ref": j.result_ref,
    })))
}

//! Concrete application state: wires infrastructure adapters to application
//! services exactly once, at startup. Handlers only ever see this typed state.

use std::sync::Arc;

use medassist_application::{
    ai::{ClinicalDocumentationEngine, LanguageModelProvider},
    audit::{AuditLister, AuditService},
    auth::AuthService,
    documents::DocumentService,
    encounters::EncounterService,
    notes::NoteService,
    patients::PatientService,
    ports::{AuditSink, BlobStore},
    templates::TemplateService,
    transcription::TranscriptionService,
};
use medassist_infrastructure::{
    ai_double::{DoubleEngine, DoubleLlm},
    ai_ollama::OllamaLlm,
    blob_fs::FsBlobStore,
    pg_repos::{
        PgAudit, PgCodes, PgConsents, PgDocuments, PgEncounters, PgJobs, PgNotes, PgPatients,
        PgSessions, PgTemplates, PgTranscriptions, PgUsers,
    },
};

/// Which documentation engine is active (resolved from env at startup).
pub enum EngineChoice {
    Ollama(Arc<OllamaLlm>),
    /// Clearly-labelled test double — NOT a real AI engine.
    Double(Arc<DoubleEngine>),
}

pub struct Inner {
    pub patients: PatientService<PgPatients, Arc<dyn AuditSink>>,
    pub encounters: EncounterService<PgEncounters, PgPatients, Arc<dyn AuditSink>>,
    pub notes: NoteService<PgNotes, PgEncounters, Arc<dyn AuditSink>>,
    pub documents: DocumentService<PgDocuments, Arc<dyn BlobStore>, PgPatients, Arc<dyn AuditSink>>,
    pub transcriptions: TranscriptionService<PgTranscriptions, PgConsents, Arc<dyn AuditSink>>,
    pub templates: TemplateService<PgTemplates>,
    pub audit: AuditService<AuditReader>,
    pub auth: AuthService<PgUsers, PgSessions, PgAudit>,
    pub jobs: PgJobs,
    pub codes: PgCodes,
    pub blobs: Arc<dyn BlobStore>,
    pub sink: Arc<dyn AuditSink>,
    pub engine: EngineChoice,
    pub llm_id: String,
    pub llm_external: bool,
}

/// Reader adapter implementing the application's `AuditLister` port on top of
/// the Postgres audit table.
pub struct AuditReader(pub PgAudit);

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl AuditLister for AuditReader {
    async fn list(
        &self,
        org_id: uuid::Uuid,
        limit: i64,
        offset: i64,
    ) -> Result<(Vec<medassist_application::ports::AuditEvent>, i64), medassist_application::AppError>
    {
        use sqlx::Row;
        let rows = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT time, actor_user_id, action, resource_type, resource_id, outcome, metadata,
                    COUNT(*) OVER() AS total
             FROM audit_events WHERE org_id=$1
             ORDER BY time DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3",
        )
        .bind(org_id)
        .bind(limit)
        .bind(offset)
        .fetch_all(self.0.pool())
        .await
        .map_err(medassist_application::AppError::storage)?;

        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());
        let mut total = 0i64;
        for r in rows {
            use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
            total = r.get::<i64, _>("total");
            let outcome = match r.get::<String, _>("outcome").as_str() {
                "success" => medassist_application::ports::AuditOutcome::Success,
                "failure" => medassist_application::ports::AuditOutcome::Failure,
                _ => medassist_application::ports::AuditOutcome::Denied,
            };
            out.push(medassist_application::ports::AuditEvent {
                time: r.get::<DateTime<Utc>, _>("time"),
                actor_user_id: r.get::<Option<uuid::Uuid>, _>("actor_user_id"),
                org_id: Some(org_id),
                action: r.get::<String, _>("action"),
                resource_type: r.get::<String, _>("resource_type"),
                resource_id: r.get::<Option<uuid::Uuid>, _>("resource_id"),
                outcome,
                metadata: r.get::<serde_json::Value, _>("metadata"),
            });
        }
        Ok((out, total))
    }

}

pub type AppState = Arc<Inner>;

/// Build the wired application state. The DB pool must already be migrated.
pub fn build_state(
    pool: sqlx::PgPool,
    blob_root: &std::path::Path,
    ollama_base: Option<String>,
    ollama_model: Option<String>,
) -> AppState {
    let audit = PgAudit::new(pool.clone());
    let patients_repo = PgPatients(pool.clone());
    let encounters_repo = PgEncounters(pool.clone());
    let docs_repo = PgDocuments(pool.clone());
    let blobs = Arc::new(FsBlobStore::new(blob_root));
    let sink: Arc<dyn AuditSink> = Arc::new(audit.clone());

    let (engine, llm_id, llm_external) = match (&ollama_base, &ollama_model) {
        (Some(base), Some(model)) => {
            let llm = Arc::new(OllamaLlm::new(base.clone(), model.clone()));
            (
                EngineChoice::Ollama(llm.clone()),
                llm.id(),
                medassist_application::ai::LanguageModelProvider::is_external(&*llm),
            )
        }
        _ => {
            tracing::warn!(
                "MEDASSIST_OLLAMA_BASE_URL/MODEL not set: using TEST DOUBLE engine. \
                 It is NOT a real AI model and is intended for development/testing only."
            );
            let d = Arc::new(DoubleEngine(DoubleLlm::new()));
            (
                EngineChoice::Double(d.clone()),
                medassist_application::ai::ClinicalDocumentationEngine::id(&*d),
                false,
            )
        }
    };

    Arc::new(Inner {
        patients: PatientService::new(patients_repo.clone(), sink.clone()),
        encounters: EncounterService::new(encounters_repo.clone(), patients_repo.clone(), sink.clone()),
        notes: NoteService::new(PgNotes(pool.clone()), encounters_repo, sink.clone()),
        documents: DocumentService::new(docs_repo, blobs.clone() as Arc<dyn BlobStore>, patients_repo, sink.clone()),
        transcriptions: TranscriptionService::new(
            PgTranscriptions(pool.clone()),
            PgConsents(pool.clone()),
            sink.clone(),
        ),
        templates: TemplateService::new(PgTemplates(pool.clone())),
        audit: AuditService::new(AuditReader(PgAudit::new(pool.clone()))),
        auth: AuthService::new(
            PgUsers(pool.clone()),
            PgSessions(pool.clone()),
            PgAudit::new(pool.clone()),
        ),
        jobs: PgJobs(pool.clone()),
        codes: PgCodes(pool),
        blobs,
        sink,
        engine,
        llm_id,
        llm_external,
    })
}

//! AI documentation assistance: provider-agnostic contracts plus the
//! anti-hallucination guardrails that turn a raw model answer into a *safe*
//! draft (provenance-tagged, gap-flagged, never auto-finalised).

use std::time::Duration;

use async_trait::async_trait;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

use medassist_domain::note::{NoteBody, SoapContent, SoapSection};
use medassist_domain::prov::{Provenance, ProvenanceKind, SourceRef};

use crate::errors::AppError;

/// Input to a documentation-draft job. Only *authorised* context is included;
/// the service layer is responsible for selecting it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DocumentationRequest {
    pub job_id: Uuid,
    pub org_id: Uuid,
    pub patient_id: Uuid,
    pub encounter_id: Uuid,
    /// Sections requested by the template.
    pub sections: Vec<String>,
    /// Transcript text (if consented and available).
    pub transcript: Option<String>,
    /// Structured clinical facts already in the record (chief complaint,
    /// vitals as text, prior confirmed problems) — passed as labelled lines.
    pub authorised_context: Vec<String>,
}

/// Raw engine response: section texts only. The engine NEVER returns status,
/// finalisation or permission information.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct EngineDraft {
    pub subjective: Option<String>,
    pub objective: Option<String>,
    pub assessment: Option<String>,
    pub plan: Option<String>,
    /// Explicit gaps reported by the engine ("information not available").
    pub flagged_gaps: Vec<String>,
    /// Model identifier as reported by the provider (best effort).
    pub model: Option<String>,
}

/// Low-level language-model port. Implementations: local engines or explicitly
/// configured remote providers. `timeout`/cancellation are enforced by the
/// caller (job worker), engines must be cancel-safe.
#[async_trait]
pub trait LanguageModelProvider: Send + Sync {
    /// Human-readable engine id, e.g. "ollama/qwen2.5:7b" or "test-double".
    fn id(&self) -> String;
    /// Whether this provider sends data OUTSIDE the deployment boundary.
    fn is_external(&self) -> bool;
    async fn health(&self) -> Result<bool, AppError>;
    async fn generate_draft(
        &self,
        req: &DocumentationRequest,
        timeout: Duration,
    ) -> Result<EngineDraft, AppError>;
}

/// High-level clinical documentation engine port (may wrap an LLM plus rules).
#[async_trait]
pub trait ClinicalDocumentationEngine: Send + Sync {
    fn id(&self) -> String;
    async fn generate(&self, req: &DocumentationRequest) -> Result<NoteBody, AppError>;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Guardrail validation
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Numbers (integers/decimals) extracted from generated text, used to check
/// that the model did not invent measurements/doses absent from input.
fn numbers_in(text: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    let bytes: Vec<char> = text.chars().collect();
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < bytes.len() {
        if bytes[i].is_ascii_digit() {
            let mut num = String::new();
            while i < bytes.len() && (bytes[i].is_ascii_digit() || matches!(bytes[i], '.' | ',')) {
                // don't swallow trailing punctuation like "3." at sentence end
                if (bytes[i] == '.' || bytes[i] == ',')
                    && (i + 1 >= bytes.len() || !bytes[i + 1].is_ascii_digit())
                {
                    break;
                }
                num.push(bytes[i]);
                i += 1;
            }
            out.push(num.replace(',', "."));
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    out
}

/// Digit-only view of a string — used for grounding checks so that unit or
/// punctuation differences ("130x85" vs "130 mmHg") don't cause false hits.
fn digits_only(text: &str) -> String {
    text.chars().filter(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).collect()
}

/// Validate an [`EngineDraft`] against its inputs. This is a *data-provenance*
/// guardrail, NOT a clinical validation: schema/number checks cannot certify
/// medical correctness. It blocks obvious fabrications of quantities and dates
/// and forces every produced section to carry AI provenance + unreviewed flag.
pub fn validate_draft(
    draft: &EngineDraft,
    req: &DocumentationRequest,
) -> Result<NoteBody, Vec<String>> {
    let mut violations = Vec::new();
    let haystack_text: String = {
        let mut h = req.transcript.clone().unwrap_or_default();
        h.push('\n');
        h.push_str(&req.authorised_context.join("\n"));
        h.to_lowercase()
    };
    let haystack_digits = digits_only(&haystack_text);

    let sections = [
        ("subjective", &draft.subjective),
        ("objective", &draft.objective),
        ("assessment", &draft.assessment),
        ("plan", &draft.plan),
    ];

    for (name, content) in sections.iter().filter(|(_, c)| c.as_deref().map(|x| !x.trim().is_empty()).unwrap_or(false)) {
        let text = content.as_ref().unwrap();
        // Every numeric token generated must have been grounded in the input.
        for n in numbers_in(text) {
            let nd = digits_only(&n);
            if !nd.is_empty() && !haystack_digits.contains(&nd) {
                violations.push(format!("{name}: fabricated quantity '{n}' not present in authorised input"));
            }
        }
        // ISO-like or dd/mm/yyyy date shapes must also be grounded.
        for tok in extract_date_like(text) {
            let td = digits_only(&tok);
            if !haystack_digits.contains(&td) {
                violations.push(format!("{name}: fabricated date '{tok}' not present in authorised input"));
            }
        }
    }

    if !violations.is_empty() {
        return Err(violations);
    }

    let job_src = SourceRef::AiJob { id: req.job_id, model: draft.model.clone() };
    let mk = |t: Option<String>| -> Option<SoapSection> {
        t.filter(|x| !x.trim().is_empty()).map(|text| SoapSection {
            text,
            provenance: Provenance::new(ProvenanceKind::AiSuggestion, job_src.clone()),
            reviewed: false, // human review is mandatory before finalisation
        })
    };

    Ok(NoteBody::Soap(SoapContent {
        subjective: mk(draft.subjective.clone()),
        objective: mk(draft.objective.clone()),
        assessment: mk(draft.assessment.clone()),
        plan: mk(draft.plan.clone()),
    }))
}

fn extract_date_like(text: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for word in text.split_whitespace() {
        let w: String = word.trim_matches(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit() && c != '/' && c != '-').into();
        let has_digits = w.chars().any(|c| c.is_ascii_digit());
        let sep = (w.contains('/') || w.contains('-')) && has_digits;
        // crude shape check: contains at least two separators groups
        if sep && w.len() >= 8 {
            out.push(w);
        }
    }
    out
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn req() -> DocumentationRequest {
        DocumentationRequest {
            job_id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            org_id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            patient_id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            encounter_id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            sections: vec!["subjective".into(), "objective".into(), "assessment".into(), "plan".into()],
            transcript: Some("paciente com cefaleia ha 3 dias. pa 130x85 mmhg".into()),
            authorised_context: vec!["queixa: dor de cabeça".into()],
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn accepts_draft_groundedin_input() {
        let d = EngineDraft {
            subjective: Some("cefaleia há 3 dias".into()),
            objective: Some("PA 130x85 mmHg".into()),
            assessment: Some("cefaleia tensional relatada".into()),
            plan: Some("retorno em 3 dias".into()),
            flagged_gaps: vec!["exames laboratoriais indisponíveis".into()],
            model: Some("test-double".into()),
        };
        let body = validate_draft(&d, &req()).expect("should pass");
        match body {
            NoteBody::Soap(s) => {
                assert_eq!(s.plan.as_ref().unwrap().provenance.kind, ProvenanceKind::AiSuggestion);
                assert!(!s.plan.as_ref().unwrap().reviewed);
                assert!(!s.unreviewed_sections().is_empty());
            }
            _ => panic!(),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_fabricated_numbers_and_dates() {
        let d = EngineDraft {
            subjective: Some("dor intensa 10/10".into()),
            objective: Some("PA 180x110 mmHg".into()),
            assessment: Some("hipertensão grade 3 desde 2019-05-04".into()),
            plan: Some("losartana 100 mg 2x ao dia".into()),
            flagged_gaps: vec![],
            model: None,
        };
        let errs = validate_draft(&d, &req()).unwrap_err();
        let joined = errs.join(";");
        assert!(joined.contains("fabricated quantity '180'"), "{joined}");
        assert!(joined.contains("fabricated quantity '100'"), "{joined}");
        assert!(joined.contains("fabricated date '2019-05-04'"), "{joined}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn number_tokenizer_handles_decimal_commas() {
        assert_eq!(numbers_in("dose 2,5 mg e PA 130x85."), ["2.5", "130", "85"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn empty_sections_are_skipped_not_flagged_as_violation() {
        let d = EngineDraft::default();
        let body = validate_draft(&d, &req()).unwrap();
        match body {
            NoteBody::Soap(s) => assert_eq!(s.missing_sections().len(), 4),
            _ => panic!(),
        }
    }
}

===== FILE 20/58: crates/medassist-application/src/audit.rs (1107 bytes) =====
//! Audit query use-case (read side). Writes happen through the `AuditSink`
//! port in every service; this exposes authorised listing for compliance UIs.

use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::errors::{AppError, Actor};
use crate::ports::AuditEvent;

/// In-memory + repository-backed audit reader is provided by infrastructure;
/// the application defines the access policy: only admins may list events,
/// and only within their own organisation.
pub struct AuditService<S: AuditLister> {
    pub lister: S,
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
pub trait AuditLister: Send + Sync {
    async fn list(&self, org_id: Uuid, limit: i64, offset: i64) -> Result<(Vec<AuditEvent>, i64), AppError>;
}

impl<S: AuditLister> AuditService<S> {
    pub fn new(lister: S) -> Self {
        Self { lister }
    }

    pub async fn list(&self, actor: &Actor, limit: i64, offset: i64) -> Result<(Vec<AuditEvent>, i64), AppError> {
        if !actor.is_admin() {
            return Err(AppError::Forbidden("audit events are visible to administrators only"));
        }
        self.lister.list(actor.org_id, limit, offset).await
    }
}

===== FILE 21/58: crates/medassist-application/src/auth.rs (13281 bytes) =====
//! Authentication use-case: Argon2id password hashing, constant-time verify,
//! lockout after repeated failures, opaque session tokens (only their SHA-256
//! hash is stored). The real HTTP wiring lives in the API crate.

use argon2::{
    password_hash::{rand_core::OsRng, PasswordHash, PasswordHasher, PasswordVerifier, SaltString},
    Algorithm, Argon2, Params, Version,
};
use chrono::{DateTime, Duration};
use sha2::{Digest as _, Sha256};
use uuid::Uuid as Guid;

use crate::errors::{AppError, Actor};
use crate::ports::{AuditEvent, AuditOutcome, AuditSink, NewUserRecord, SessionRecord, SessionRepository, UserRepository};

pub const MAX_FAILED_LOGINS: i32 = 5;
pub const LOCKOUT_MINUTES: i64 = 15;
pub const SESSION_TTL_DAYS: i64 = 7;
/// Minimum accepted password length (NIST SP 800-63B guidance: >= 8).
pub const MIN_PASSWORD_LEN: usize = 8;

pub fn argon2id() -> Argon2<'static> {
    // m=19MiB, t=2, p=1 — OWASP-ish parameters for Argon2id.
    Argon2::new(Algorithm::Argon2id, Version::V0x13, Params::default())
}

pub fn hash_password(password: &str) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    let salt = SaltString::generate(&mut OsRng);
    argon2id()
        .hash_password(password.as_bytes(), &salt)
        .map(|h| h.to_string())
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Unprocessable(format!("password hashing failed: {e}")))
}

pub fn verify_password(password: &str, hash: &str) -> bool {
    match PasswordHash::new(hash) {
        Ok(parsed) => argon2id().verify_password(password.as_bytes(), &parsed).is_ok(),
        Err(_) => false,
    }
}

pub fn validate_password_strength(password: &str) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    if password.len() < MIN_PASSWORD_LEN {
        return Err(AppError::Unprocessable(format!(
            "password must have at least {MIN_PASSWORD_LEN} characters"
        )));
    }
    if password.len() > 256 {
        return Err(AppError::Unprocessable("password too long".into()));
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Generate a cryptographically random opaque session token.
pub fn generate_session_token() -> String {
    use rand::Rng;
    let mut bytes = [0u8; 32];
    rand::rngs::OsRng.fill(&mut bytes);
    hex_encode(&bytes)
}

/// SHA-256 hex of the token — sessions are looked up by hash so a database
/// leak cannot be replayed directly.
pub fn hash_token(token: &str) -> String {
    let mut h = Sha256::new();
    h.update(token.as_bytes());
    hex_encode(&h.finalize())
}

fn hex_encode(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    use std::fmt::Write as _;
    let mut s = String::with_capacity(bytes.len() * 2);
    for b in bytes {
        let _ = write!(s, "{b:02x}");
    }
    s
}

pub struct AuthService<U: UserRepository, S: SessionRepository, A: AuditSink> {
    pub users: U,
    pub sessions: S,
    pub audit: A,
}

impl<U: UserRepository, S: SessionRepository, A: AuditSink> AuthService<U, S, A> {
    pub fn new(users: U, sessions: S, audit: A) -> Self {
        Self { users, sessions, audit }
    }

    pub async fn register_first_admin(
        &self,
        org_id: Guid,
        email: &str,
        password: &str,
    ) -> Result<Guid, AppError> {
        validate_password_strength(password)?;
        let email = normalise_email(email)?;
        if self.users.find_by_email(&email).await?.is_some() {
            return Err(AppError::Conflict("email already registered".into()));
        }
        let hash = hash_password(password)?;
        self.users
            .insert(&NewUserRecord { org_id, email, password_hash: hash, roles: vec!["admin".into(), "practitioner".into()] })
            .await
    }

    /// Login returns `(session_token, actor)`. On bad credentials it performs
    /// a dummy hash to reduce user-enumeration timing differences.
    pub async fn login(
        &self,
        email: &str,
        password: &str,
        now: DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
    ) -> Result<(String, Actor), AppError> {
        let email = normalise_email(email)?;
        let user = match self.users.find_by_email(&email).await? {
            Some(u) => u,
            None => {
                let _ = verify_password(password, "$argon2id$v=19$m=19456,t=2,p=1$AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA$AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA");
                self.audit(None, "auth.login", AuditOutcome::Denied).await;
                return Err(AppError::Unauthenticated);
            }
        };

        if let Some(until) = user.locked_until {
            if until > now {
                self.audit(Some(user.id), "auth.login.locked", AuditOutcome::Denied).await;
                return Err(AppError::Unauthenticated);
            }
        }

        if !verify_password(password, &user.password_hash) {
            self.users.register_login_failure(user.id).await?;
            let fails = user.failed_logins + 1;
            if fails >= MAX_FAILED_LOGINS {
                self.users.lock_until(user.id, now + Duration::minutes(LOCKOUT_MINUTES)).await?;
                self.audit(Some(user.id), "auth.login.lockout_triggered", AuditOutcome::Failure).await;
            }
            self.audit(Some(user.id), "auth.login.bad_password", AuditOutcome::Denied).await;
            return Err(AppError::Unauthenticated);
        }

        self.users.register_login_success(user.id, now).await?;
        let token = generate_session_token();
        let session = SessionRecord {
            token_hash: hash_token(&token),
            user_id: user.id,
            org_id: user.org_id,
            roles: user.roles.clone(),
            expires_at: now + Duration::days(SESSION_TTL_DAYS),
            revoked_at: None,
        };
        self.sessions.insert(&session).await?;
        self.audit(Some(user.id), "auth.login", AuditOutcome::Success).await;
        Ok((token, Actor { user_id: user.id, org_id: user.org_id, roles: user.roles }))
    }

    pub async fn resolve_session(&self, token: &str, now: DateTime<chrono::Utc>) -> Result<Actor, AppError> {
        let hash = hash_token(token);
        match self.sessions.find_by_token_hash(&hash).await? {
            Some(s) if s.revoked_at.is_none() && s.expires_at > now => {
                Ok(Actor { user_id: s.user_id, org_id: s.org_id, roles: s.roles })
            }
            _ => Err(AppError::Unauthenticated),
        }
    }

    pub async fn logout(&self, token: &str, now: DateTime<chrono::Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let hash = hash_token(token);
        self.sessions.revoke(&hash, now).await
    }

    async fn audit(&self, user_id: Option<Guid>, action: &str, outcome: AuditOutcome) {
        self.audit
            .record(AuditEvent {
                time: chrono::Utc::now(),
                actor_user_id: user_id,
                org_id: None,
                action: action.into(),
                resource_type: "auth".into(),
                resource_id: None,
                outcome,
                metadata: serde_json::json!({}),
            })
            .await;
    }
}

pub fn normalise_email(email: &str) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    let e = email.trim().to_lowercase();
    let ok = e.len() <= 254 && e.contains('@') && e.rfind('.') > Some(e.find('@').unwrap_or(0));
    if !ok {
        return Err(AppError::Unprocessable("invalid email address".into()));
    }
    Ok(e)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::collections::HashMap;
    use std::sync::Mutex;

    #[derive(Default)]
    struct MemUsers {
        by_email: Mutex<HashMap<String, crate::ports::UserRecord>>,
    }

    #[async_trait::async_trait]
    impl UserRepository for MemUsers {
        async fn find_by_email(&self, email: &str) -> Result<Option<crate::ports::UserRecord>, AppError> {
            Ok(self.by_email.lock().unwrap().get(email).cloned())
        }
        async fn insert(&self, u: &NewUserRecord) -> Result<Guid, AppError> {
            let id = Guid::new_v4();
            self.by_email.lock().unwrap().insert(
                u.email.clone(),
                crate::ports::UserRecord {
                    id,
                    org_id: u.org_id,
                    email: u.email.clone(),
                    password_hash: u.password_hash.clone(),
                    roles: u.roles.clone(),
                    failed_logins: 0,
                    locked_until: None,
                },
            );
            Ok(id)
        }
        async fn update_password_hash(&self, _id: Guid, _hash: String) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn register_login_success(&self, _id: Guid, _now: DateTime<chrono::Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn register_login_failure(&self, id: Guid) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            let mut g = self.by_email.lock().unwrap();
            if let Some(u) = g.values_mut().find(|u| u.id == id) {
                u.failed_logins += 1;
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn lock_until(&self, id: Guid, until: DateTime<chrono::Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            let mut g = self.by_email.lock().unwrap();
            if let Some(u) = g.values_mut().find(|u| u.id == id) {
                u.locked_until = Some(until);
            }
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    #[derive(Default)]
    struct MemSessions(Mutex<HashMap<String, SessionRecord>>);

    #[async_trait::async_trait]
    impl SessionRepository for MemSessions {
        async fn insert(&self, s: &SessionRecord) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            self.0.lock().unwrap().insert(s.token_hash.clone(), s.clone());
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn find_by_token_hash(&self, h: &str) -> Result<Option<SessionRecord>, AppError> {
            Ok(self.0.lock().unwrap().get(h).cloned())
        }
        async fn revoke(&self, h: &str, now: DateTime<chrono::Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            if let Some(s) = self.0.lock().unwrap().get_mut(h) {
                s.revoked_at = Some(now);
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn revoke_all_for_user(&self, _u: Guid, _now: DateTime<chrono::Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    #[derive(Default)]
    struct MemAudit(Mutex<Vec<AuditEvent>>);
    #[async_trait::async_trait]
    impl AuditSink for MemAudit {
        async fn record(&self, ev: AuditEvent) {
            self.0.lock().unwrap().push(ev);
        }
    }

    fn svc() -> AuthService<MemUsers, MemSessions, MemAudit> {
        AuthService::new(MemUsers::default(), MemSessions::default(), MemAudit::default())
    }

    #[test]
    fn argon2id_hash_verifies_and_rejects_wrong_password() {
        let h = hash_password("correct horse battery staple").unwrap();
        assert!(h.starts_with("$argon2id$"), "must be argon2id, got {h}");
        assert!(verify_password("correct horse battery staple", &h));
        assert!(!verify_password("wrong", &h));
    }

    #[test]
    fn weak_passwords_rejected() {
        assert!(validate_password_strength("short1!").is_err());
        assert!(validate_password_strength("longenough1").is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn email_normalisation_and_validation() {
        assert_eq!(normalise_email("  Ana.Silva@Example.COM ").unwrap(), "ana.silva@example.com");
        assert!(normalise_email("not-an-email").is_err());
        assert!(normalise_email("a@b").is_err());
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn login_creates_resolvable_session_and_logout_revokes_it() {
        let s = svc();
        let org = Guid::new_v4();
        s.register_first_admin(org, "admin@clinic.test", "sup3r-s3cret-pw").await.unwrap();
        let (token, actor) = s.login("ADMIN@clinic.test", "sup3r-s3cret-pw", chrono::Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(actor.org_id, org);
        let resolved = s.resolve_session(&token, chrono::Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(resolved.user_id, actor.user_id);
        s.logout(&token, chrono::Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(s.resolve_session(&token, chrono::Utc::now()).await, Err(AppError::Unauthenticated)));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn lockout_after_max_failures() {
        let s = svc();
        let org = Guid::new_v4();
        s.register_first_admin(org, "u@clinic.test", "good-password-1").await.unwrap();
        for _ in 0..MAX_FAILED_LOGINS {
            assert!(s.login("u@clinic.test", "bad-password-x", chrono::Utc::now()).await.is_err());
        }
        // Even the correct password is refused while locked.
        let later = chrono::Utc::now() + Duration::seconds(1);
        let err = s.login("u@clinic.test", "good-password-1", later).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Unauthenticated));
        let events = s.audit.0.lock().unwrap();
        assert!(events.iter().any(|e| e.action == "auth.login.lockout_triggered"));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn expired_session_is_rejected() {
        let s = svc();
        let org = Guid::new_v4();
        s.register_first_admin(org, "e@clinic.test", "another-good-pw").await.unwrap();
        let (token, _) = s.login("e@clinic.test", "another-good-pw", chrono::Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        let far_future = chrono::Utc::now() + Duration::days(SESSION_TTL_DAYS + 1);
        assert!(matches!(s.resolve_session(&token, far_future).await, Err(AppError::Unauthenticated)));
    }
}
