//! Document import use-cases: validation, hashing, persistence of metadata,
//! blob storage and extraction job creation. Originals are immutable;
//! extractions stay unreviewed until a professional confirms them.

use chrono::Utc;
use sha2::{Digest as _, Sha256};
use uuid::Uuid;

use medassist_domain::document::{ClinicalDocument, ProcessingState};
use medassist_domain::DomainError;

use crate::errors::{AppError, Actor};
use crate::ports::{AuditEvent, AuditOutcome, AuditSink, BlobStore, DocumentRepository, PatientRepository};

pub struct DocumentService<D: DocumentRepository, B: BlobStore, P: PatientRepository, A: AuditSink> {
    pub docs: D,
    pub blobs: B,
    pub patients: P,
    pub audit: A,
}

impl<D: DocumentRepository, B: BlobStore, P: PatientRepository, A: AuditSink> DocumentService<D, B, P, A> {
    pub fn new(docs: D, blobs: B, patients: P, audit: A) -> Self {
        Self { docs, blobs, patients, audit }
    }

    /// Import a document for a patient. Validates filename/mime/size, computes
    /// SHA-256 over the received bytes, stores the original immutably and
    /// records metadata in state `Pending`.
    pub async fn import(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        patient_id: Uuid,
        encounter_id: Option<Uuid>,
        filename: String,
        content_type: String,
        bytes: Vec<u8>,
    ) -> Result<ClinicalDocument, AppError> {
        // Tenant check first (also prevents cross-org probing via hashes).
        // A missing patient for this actor means either it does not exist or
        // belongs to another org — both are answered identically (403) so the
        // API never leaks existence across tenants.
        let patient = match self.patients.get(actor, patient_id).await? {
            Some(p) => p,
            None => {
                self.audit(actor, Some(patient_id), "document.import", AuditOutcome::Denied).await;
                return Err(AppError::Forbidden("patient not visible to you"));
            }
        };
        if patient.org_id != actor.org_id && !actor.is_admin() {
            self.audit(actor, Some(patient_id), "document.import", AuditOutcome::Denied).await;
            return Err(AppError::Forbidden("patient is outside your organisation"));
        }

        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
        hasher.update(&bytes);
        let sha256 = hasher.finalize().iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect::<String>();

        let kind = ClinicalDocument::validate_new(&filename, &content_type, bytes.len() as u64, &sha256)
            .map_err(DomainError::from)?;

        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let doc = ClinicalDocument {
            id,
            org_id: patient.org_id,
            patient_id,
            encounter_id,
            kind,
            filename: filename.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or(&filename).to_owned(),
            content_type,
            size_bytes: bytes.len() as u64,
            sha256,
            received_at: None,
            state: ProcessingState::Pending,
            error: None,
            uploaded_by: actor.user_id,
            created_at: Utc::now(),
        };
        self.blobs.put(patient.org_id, id, &bytes).await?;
        self.docs.insert(&doc).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(id), "document.import", AuditOutcome::Success).await;
        Ok(doc)
    }

    /// List documents attached to a patient (newest first), org-scoped.
    pub async fn list_for_patient(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        patient_id: Uuid,
        limit: i64,
        offset: i64,
    ) -> Result<crate::ports::Page<ClinicalDocument>, AppError> {
        // Authorised patient read enforces visibility before listing.
        let _p = match self.patients.get(actor, patient_id).await? {
            Some(p) => p,
            None => return Err(AppError::NotFound("patient")),
        };
        self.docs.list_for_patient(actor, patient_id, limit, offset).await
    }

    pub async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<ClinicalDocument, AppError> {
        match self.docs.get(actor, id).await? {
            Some(d) => Ok(d),
            None => Err(AppError::NotFound("document")),
        }
    }

    pub async fn download(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
        let d = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        let key = format!("{}/{}", d.org_id, d.id);
        self.blobs.get(&key).await
    }

    /// Mark extraction results as reviewed by a professional.
    pub async fn mark_reviewed(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        if !actor.has_role("practitioner") && !actor.is_admin() {
            self.audit(actor, Some(id), "document.review", AuditOutcome::Denied).await;
            return Err(AppError::Forbidden("requires practitioner role"));
        }
        let d = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        if d.state != ProcessingState::Extracted {
            return Err(AppError::Unprocessable(format!("cannot review document in state {:?}", d.state)));
        }
        self.docs.set_state(actor, id, ProcessingState::Reviewed, None).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(id), "document.review", AuditOutcome::Success).await;
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn audit(&self, actor: &Actor, resource_id: Option<Uuid>, action: &str, outcome: AuditOutcome) {
        self.audit
            .record(AuditEvent {
                time: Utc::now(),
                actor_user_id: Some(actor.user_id),
                org_id: Some(actor.org_id),
                action: action.into(),
                resource_type: "document".into(),
                resource_id,
                outcome,
                metadata: serde_json::json!({}),
            })
            .await;
    }
}

            Ok(())

        let a = actor_with_org(org);
        let p = seed_patient(&s, org);
        let d = s.import(&a, p.id, None, "r.txt".into(), "text/plain".into(), b"resultado".to_vec()).await.unwrap();
        // pending -> cannot review
        assert!(s.mark_reviewed(&a, d.id).await.is_err());
        // extracted but recorder lacks role
        s.docs.set_state(&a, d.id, ProcessingState::Extracted, None).await.unwrap();
        let err = s.mark_reviewed(&a, d.id).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Forbidden(_)));
        // practitioner can
        let prac = Actor { user_id: a.user_id, org_id: org, roles: vec!["practitioner".into()] };
        s.mark_reviewed(&prac, d.id).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(s.get(&prac, d.id).await.unwrap().state, ProcessingState::Reviewed);
    }
}

===== FILE 23/58: crates/medassist-application/src/encounters.rs (4267 bytes) =====
//! Encounter use-cases with lifecycle transitions and org isolation checks.

use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use uuid::Uuid;

use medassist_domain::encounter::{Encounter, EncounterStatus, NewEncounter};
use medassist_domain::DomainError;

use crate::errors::{AppError, Actor};
use crate::ports::{AuditEvent, AuditOutcome, AuditSink, EncounterRepository, Page, PatientRepository};

pub struct EncounterService<R: EncounterRepository, P: PatientRepository, A: AuditSink> {
    pub repo: R,
    pub patients: P,
    pub audit: A,
}

impl<R: EncounterRepository, P: PatientRepository, A: AuditSink> EncounterService<R, P, A> {
    pub fn new(repo: R, patients: P, audit: A) -> Self {
        Self { repo, patients, audit }
    }

    pub async fn create(&self, actor: &Actor, input: NewEncounter, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<Encounter, AppError> {
        if input.org_id != actor.org_id && !actor.is_admin() {
            self.audit(actor, None, "encounter.create", "denied").await;
            return Err(AppError::Forbidden("cannot create encounters for another organisation"));
        }
        input.validate()?;
        // The patient must exist *within the actor's org* — a random id from
        // another tenant must not be linkable.
        let patient = self.patients.get(actor, input.patient_id).await?.ok_or(AppError::Unauthorized)?;
        if patient.org_id != input.org_id {
            return Err(DomainError::validation("patient_id", "patient belongs to a different organisation").into());
        }
        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let enc = input.into_encounter(id, now);
        self.repo.insert(&enc).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(enc.id), "encounter.create", "success").await;
        Ok(enc)
    }

    pub async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Encounter, AppError> {
        match self.repo.get(actor, id).await? {
            Some(e) => Ok(e),
            None => {
                self.audit(actor, Some(id), "encounter.read", "denied").await;
                Err(AppError::NotFound("encounter"))
            }
        }
    }

    pub async fn list_for_patient(&self, actor: &Actor, patient_id: Uuid, limit: i64, offset: i64) -> Result<Page<Encounter>, AppError> {
        self.repo.list_for_patient(actor, patient_id, limit, offset).await
    }

    pub async fn transition(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        id: Uuid,
        to: EncounterStatus,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Encounter, AppError> {
        let mut e = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        e.transition(to, now)?;
        self.repo.update(&e).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(e.id), &format!("encounter.transition.{}", to.as_str()), "success").await;
        Ok(e)
    }

    pub async fn update_chief_complaint(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        id: Uuid,
        complaint: String,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Encounter, AppError> {
        let mut e = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        if matches!(e.status, EncounterStatus::Finalised | EncounterStatus::Cancelled) {
            return Err(DomainError::ImmutableField.into());
        }
        if complaint.chars().count() > 2000 {
            return Err(DomainError::validation("chief_complaint", "must be at most 2000 characters").into());
        }
        e.chief_complaint = Some(complaint.trim().to_owned()).filter(|c| !c.is_empty());
        e.updated_at = now;
        self.repo.update(&e).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(e.id), "encounter.update", "success").await;
        Ok(e)
    }

    async fn audit(&self, actor: &Actor, resource_id: Option<Uuid>, action: &str, outcome: &str) {
        self.audit
            .record(AuditEvent {
                time: Utc::now(),
                actor_user_id: Some(actor.user_id),
                org_id: Some(actor.org_id),
                action: action.to_owned(),
                resource_type: "encounter".to_owned(),
                resource_id,
                outcome: match outcome {
                    "success" => AuditOutcome::Success,
                    "denied" => AuditOutcome::Denied,
                    _ => AuditOutcome::Failure,
                },
                metadata: serde_json::json!({}),
            })
            .await;
    }
}

===== FILE 24/58: crates/medassist-application/src/errors.rs (3453 bytes) =====
use medassist_domain::DomainError;
use thiserror::Error;

/// Application-level error taxonomy. The API layer maps these to HTTP codes;
/// nothing here knows about HTTP.
#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum AppError {
    #[error(transparent)]
    Domain(#[from] DomainError),

    #[error("unauthenticated")]
    Unauthenticated,

    #[error("not authorised to access this resource")]
    Unauthorized,

    #[error("forbidden: {0}")]
    Forbidden(&'static str),

    #[error("not found: {0}")]
    NotFound(&'static str),

    #[error("conflict: {0}")]
    Conflict(String),

    #[error("unprocessable: {0}")]
    Unprocessable(String),

    #[error("external service error: {0}")]
    ExternalService(String),

    #[error("timeout")]
    Timeout,

    #[error("cancelled")]
    Cancelled,

    #[error("internal error")]
    Internal(#[source] Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>),
}

impl AppError {
    pub fn internal(e: impl std::error::Error + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self {
        AppError::Internal(Box::new(e))
    }

    /// Wrap an opaque storage/infrastructure error without leaking its type.
    pub fn storage(e: impl std::error::Error + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self {
        tracing::error!(error = %e, "storage error");
        AppError::Internal(Box::new(StorageError))
    }

    /// Stable machine-readable code for API error envelopes.
    pub fn code(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            AppError::Domain(DomainError::Validation { .. }) => "validation_error",
            AppError::Domain(DomainError::InvalidDateRange { .. }) => "invalid_date_range",
            AppError::Domain(DomainError::VersionConflict { .. }) => "version_conflict",
            AppError::Domain(DomainError::IllegalTransition { .. }) => "illegal_transition",
            AppError::Domain(DomainError::ImmutableField) => "immutable_field",
            AppError::Domain(DomainError::ProvenanceRequired(_)) => "provenance_required",
            AppError::Domain(DomainError::NotFound(_)) => "not_found",
            AppError::Domain(DomainError::DuplicateCandidate(_)) => "duplicate_candidate",
            AppError::Unauthenticated => "unauthenticated",
            AppError::Unauthorized => "unauthorized",
            AppError::Forbidden(_) => "forbidden",
            AppError::NotFound(_) => "not_found",
            AppError::Conflict(_) => "conflict",
            AppError::Unprocessable(_) => "unprocessable",
            AppError::ExternalService(_) => "external_service_error",
            AppError::Timeout => "timeout",
            AppError::Cancelled => "cancelled",
            AppError::Internal(_) => "internal_error",
        }
    }
}

/// Opaque marker so infrastructure failures never leak driver details to the
/// API surface (the real cause is logged, not returned).
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct StorageError;
impl std::fmt::Display for StorageError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "storage failure")
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for StorageError {}

/// Machine-facing context propagated from transport into use-cases.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Actor {
    pub user_id: uuid::Uuid,
    pub org_id: uuid::Uuid,
    pub roles: Vec<String>,
}

impl Actor {
    pub fn has_role(&self, role: &str) -> bool {
        self.roles.iter().any(|r| r == role)
    }
    pub fn is_admin(&self) -> bool {
        self.has_role("admin")
    }
}

//! Application layer: use-cases orchestrate domain invariants through *ports*
//! (traits) implemented by infrastructure. No Axum/SQLx here.
//!
//! License: GPL-3.0-or-later.

pub mod ai;
pub mod audit;
pub mod auth;
pub mod documents;
pub mod encounters;
pub mod errors;
pub mod notes;
pub mod patients;
pub mod ports;
pub mod templates;
pub mod transcription;

pub use errors::AppError;

/// Pagination defaults shared across use-cases.
pub const DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE: i64 = 25;
pub const MAX_PAGE_SIZE: i64 = 100;

pub fn clamp_page(limit: Option<i64>, offset: Option<i64>) -> (i64, i64) {
    let limit = limit.unwrap_or(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE).clamp(1, MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
    let offset = offset.unwrap_or(0).max(0);
    (limit, offset)
}

===== FILE 26/58: crates/medassist-application/src/notes.rs (15854 bytes) =====
//! Note use-cases: draft creation, conflict-safe editing (human edits are
//! never silently overwritten), section review, controlled finalisation and
//! amendments. Every mutation is snapshotted for version comparison.

use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use uuid::Uuid;

use medassist_domain::note::{Note, NoteBody};
use medassist_domain::DomainError;

use crate::errors::{AppError, Actor};
use crate::ports::{AuditEvent, AuditOutcome, AuditSink, EncounterRepository, NoteRepository};

pub struct NoteService<N: NoteRepository, E: EncounterRepository, A: AuditSink> {
    pub notes: N,
    pub encounters: E,
    pub audit: A,
}

impl<N: NoteRepository, E: EncounterRepository, A: AuditSink> NoteService<N, E, A> {
    pub fn new(notes: N, encounters: E, audit: A) -> Self {
        Self { notes, encounters, audit }
    }

    pub async fn create_draft(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        encounter_id: Uuid,
        body: NoteBody,
        template_id: Option<Uuid>,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Note, AppError> {
        let enc = self.encounters.get(actor, encounter_id).await?.ok_or(AppError::Unauthorized)?;
        if matches!(enc.status, medassist_domain::encounter::EncounterStatus::Cancelled) {
            return Err(DomainError::validation("encounter", "cannot document a cancelled encounter").into());
        }
        validate_body(&body)?;
        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let mut note = Note::new_draft(id, actor.org_id, enc.id, enc.patient_id, actor.user_id, body, now);
        note.template_id = template_id;
        self.notes.insert(&note).await?;
        self.notes.append_version_snapshot(&note, actor.user_id).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(note.id), "note.create_draft", AuditOutcome::Success).await;
        Ok(note)
    }

    pub async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Note, AppError> {
        match self.notes.get(actor, id).await? {
            Some(n) => Ok(n),
            None => {
                self.audit(actor, Some(id), "note.read", AuditOutcome::Denied).await;
                Err(AppError::NotFound("note"))
            }
        }
    }

    /// List every note belonging to an encounter (drafts included), newest
    /// first. Authorisation is enforced through the encounter read.
    pub async fn list_for_encounter(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        encounter_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Vec<Note>, AppError> {
        // 404/403 if the encounter is not visible to this actor.
        let _enc = self.encounters.get(actor, encounter_id).await?;
        let notes = self.notes.list_for_encounter(actor, encounter_id).await?;
        Ok(notes)
    }

    /// Conflict-safe edit. `expected_version` must match the stored version;
    /// otherwise [`DomainError::VersionConflict`] propagates so the client can
    /// rebase instead of clobbering a human's changes with an async AI result.
    pub async fn edit(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        id: Uuid,
        expected_version: u32,
        body: NoteBody,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Note, AppError> {
        let mut note = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        validate_body(&body)?;
        note.apply_edit(expected_version, body, now)?;
        self.notes.update_with_version_guard(&note, expected_version, actor.user_id).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(note.id), "note.edit", AuditOutcome::Success).await;
        Ok(note)
    }

    pub async fn review_section(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        id: Uuid,
        section: &str,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Note, AppError> {
        let mut note = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        let v = note.version;
        note.mark_section_reviewed(section, now)?;
        self.notes.update_with_version_guard(&note, v, actor.user_id).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(note.id), "note.review_section", AuditOutcome::Success).await;
        Ok(note)
    }

    /// Controlled finalisation. Requires professional action; gap overrides
    /// are explicitly audited so they can be reviewed later.
    pub async fn finalise(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        id: Uuid,
        override_gaps: bool,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Note, AppError> {
        if !actor.has_role("practitioner") && !actor.is_admin() {
            self.audit(actor, Some(id), "note.finalise", AuditOutcome::Denied).await;
            return Err(AppError::Forbidden("finalising notes requires the practitioner role"));
        }
        let mut note = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        let v = note.version;
        note.finalise(actor.user_id, now, override_gaps)?;
        self.notes.update_with_version_guard(&note, v, actor.user_id).await?;
        self.audit(
            actor,
            Some(note.id),
            if override_gaps { "note.finalise.with_gap_override" } else { "note.finalise" },
            AuditOutcome::Success,
        )
        .await;
        Ok(note)
    }

    /// Corrections to a finalised note create a linked amendment draft.
    pub async fn amend(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        id: Uuid,
        body: NoteBody,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Note, AppError> {
        let original = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        validate_body(&body)?;
        let amendment = original.create_amendment(Uuid::new_v4(), body, now)?;
        self.notes.insert(&amendment).await?;
        self.notes.append_version_snapshot(&amendment, actor.user_id).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(amendment.id), "note.amend", AuditOutcome::Success).await;
        Ok(amendment)
    }

    async fn audit(&self, actor: &Actor, resource_id: Option<Uuid>, action: &str, outcome: AuditOutcome) {
        self.audit
            .record(AuditEvent {
                time: Utc::now(),
                actor_user_id: Some(actor.user_id),
                org_id: Some(actor.org_id),
                action: action.to_owned(),
                resource_type: "note".to_owned(),
                resource_id,
                outcome,
                metadata: serde_json::json!({}),
            })
            .await;
    }
}

/// Structural validation of note bodies (not clinical validation). Enforces
/// provenance presence and length limits; AI content must carry its origin.
pub fn validate_body(body: &NoteBody) -> Result<(), DomainError> {
    const MAX_SECTION: usize = 50_000;
    match body {
        NoteBody::Soap(s) => {
            for sec in [&s.subjective, &s.objective, &s.assessment, &s.plan].into_iter().flatten() {
                if sec.text.chars().count() > MAX_SECTION {
                    return Err(DomainError::validation("section", format!("section exceeds {MAX_SECTION} chars")));
                }
            }
        }
        NoteBody::FreeText { text, .. } => {
            if text.chars().count() > MAX_SECTION {
                return Err(DomainError::validation("text", "note exceeds char limit"));
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::sync::Mutex;

    use medassist_domain::encounter::{Encounter, EncounterStatus, NewEncounter};
    use medassist_domain::note::SoapSection;
    use medassist_domain::prov::{Provenance, ProvenanceKind, SourceRef};
    use crate::ports::Page;

    // ---- in-memory test doubles (clearly NOT real infrastructure) ----

    #[derive(Default)]
    struct MemNotes {
        by_id: Mutex<std::collections::HashMap<Uuid, Note>>,
        versions: Mutex<Vec<(Uuid, u32)>>,
    }

    #[async_trait::async_trait]
    impl NoteRepository for MemNotes {
        async fn insert(&self, n: &Note) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            self.by_id.lock().unwrap().insert(n.id, n.clone());
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn get(&self, _a: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<Note>, AppError> {
            Ok(self.by_id.lock().unwrap().get(&id).cloned())
        }
        async fn list_for_encounter(&self, _a: &Actor, e: Uuid) -> Result<Vec<Note>, AppError> {
            Ok(self.by_id.lock().unwrap().values().filter(|n| n.encounter_id == e).cloned().collect())
        }
        async fn update_with_version_guard(&self, n: &Note, expected: u32, _by: Uuid) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            let mut g = self.by_id.lock().unwrap();
            let cur = g.get(&n.id).ok_or(AppError::NotFound("note"))?.clone();
            if cur.version != expected {
                return Err(DomainError::VersionConflict { expected, found: cur.version }.into());
            }
            g.insert(n.id, n.clone());
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn append_version_snapshot(&self, n: &Note, _by: Uuid) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            self.versions.lock().unwrap().push((n.id, n.version));
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    #[derive(Default)]
    struct MemEncounters {
        by_id: Mutex<std::collections::HashMap<Uuid, Encounter>>,
    }

    #[async_trait::async_trait]
    impl EncounterRepository for MemEncounters {
        async fn insert(&self, e: &Encounter) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            self.by_id.lock().unwrap().insert(e.id, e.clone());
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn get(&self, _a: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<Encounter>, AppError> {
            Ok(self.by_id.lock().unwrap().get(&id).cloned())
        }
        async fn list_for_patient(&self, _a: &Actor, pid: Uuid, limit: i64, offset: i64) -> Result<Page<Encounter>, AppError> {
            let items: Vec<_> = self.by_id.lock().unwrap().values().filter(|e| e.patient_id == pid).cloned().collect();
            Ok(Page { total: items.len() as i64, limit, offset, items })
        }
        async fn update(&self, e: &Encounter) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            self.by_id.lock().unwrap().insert(e.id, e.clone());
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    #[derive(Default)]
    struct MemAudit {
        events: Mutex<Vec<AuditEvent>>,
    }

    #[async_trait::async_trait]
    impl AuditSink for MemAudit {
        async fn record(&self, ev: AuditEvent) {
            self.events.lock().unwrap().push(ev);
        }
    }

    fn svc() -> NoteService<MemNotes, MemEncounters, MemAudit> {
        NoteService::new(MemNotes::default(), MemEncounters::default(), MemAudit::default())
    }

    fn actor(role: &str) -> Actor {
        Actor { user_id: Uuid::new_v4(), org_id: Uuid::new_v4(), roles: vec![role.into()] }
    }

    fn soap(a: Uuid) -> NoteBody {
        NoteBody::Soap(medassist_domain::note::SoapContent {
            subjective: Some(SoapSection::professional(a, "queixa")),
            objective: Some(SoapSection::professional(a, "achados")),
            assessment: Some(SoapSection::professional(a, "avaliação")),
            plan: Some(SoapSection::professional(a, "plano")),
        })
    }

    fn ai_section(text: &str) -> SoapSection {
        SoapSection {
            text: text.into(),
            provenance: Provenance::new(ProvenanceKind::AiSuggestion, SourceRef::AiJob { id: Uuid::new_v4(), model: Some("double".into()) }),
            reviewed: false,
        }
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn creates_draft_snapshots_and_audits() {
        let s = svc();
        let a = actor("practitioner");
        let enc = NewEncounter { org_id: a.org_id, patient_id: Uuid::new_v4(), practitioner_id: a.user_id, chief_complaint: None }
            .into_encounter(Uuid::new_v4(), Utc::now());
        s.encounters.insert(&enc).await.unwrap();
        let n = s.create_draft(&a, enc.id, soap(a.user_id), None, Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(n.status, medassist_domain::note::NoteStatus::Draft);
        assert_eq!(n.version, 1);
        assert_eq!(s.notes.versions.lock().unwrap().len(), 1);
        assert!(s.audit.events.lock().unwrap().iter().any(|e| e.action == "note.create_draft"));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn stale_async_edit_conflicts_and_preserves_human_edit() {
        let s = svc();
        let a = actor("practitioner");
        let enc = NewEncounter { org_id: a.org_id, patient_id: Uuid::new_v4(), practitioner_id: a.user_id, chief_complaint: None }
            .into_encounter(Uuid::new_v4(), Utc::now());
        s.encounters.insert(&enc).await.unwrap();
        let n = s.create_draft(&a, enc.id, soap(a.user_id), None, Utc::now()).await.unwrap();

        // Human edits first (v1 -> v2).
        let human_body = {
            let mut b = soap(a.user_id);
            if let NoteBody::Soap(x) = &mut b { x.subjective.as_mut().unwrap().text = "editado pelo humano".into(); }
            b
        };
        s.edit(&a, n.id, 1, human_body.clone(), Utc::now()).await.unwrap();

        // Async AI job tries to write using the stale version 1.
        let ai_body = {
            let mut b = soap(a.user_id);
            if let NoteBody::Soap(x) = &mut b { x.assessment = Some(ai_section("sugestão da IA")); }
            b
        };
        let err = s.edit(&a, n.id, 1, ai_body, Utc::now()).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Domain(DomainError::VersionConflict { expected: 1, found: 2 })));

        // Human content preserved.
        let stored = s.get(&a, n.id).await.unwrap();
        match &stored.body {
            NoteBody::Soap(x) => assert_eq!(x.subjective.as_ref().unwrap().text, "editado pelo humano"),
            _ => panic!(),
        }
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn finalise_requires_practitioner_role() {
        let s = svc();
        let rec = actor("recorder");
        let prac = actor("practitioner");
        let enc = NewEncounter { org_id: prac.org_id, patient_id: Uuid::new_v4(), practitioner_id: prac.user_id, chief_complaint: None }
            .into_encounter(Uuid::new_v4(), Utc::now());
        s.encounters.insert(&enc).await.unwrap();
        // recorder cannot finalise — forbidden + denied audit.
        let n = s.notes.get(&prac, enc.id).await; // unrelated call to keep types simple
        let _ = n;
        let draft = s.create_draft(&prac, enc.id, soap(prac.user_id), None, Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        let err = s.finalise(&rec, draft.id, false, Utc::now()).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Forbidden(_)));
        // but the practitioner can.
        s.finalise(&prac, draft.id, false, Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn finalised_note_edits_are_immutable_and_amendment_links() {
        let s = svc();
        let a = actor("practitioner");
        let enc = NewEncounter { org_id: a.org_id, patient_id: Uuid::new_v4(), practitioner_id: a.user_id, chief_complaint: None }
            .into_encounter(Uuid::new_v4(), Utc::now());
        s.encounters.insert(&enc).await.unwrap();
        let n = s.create_draft(&a, enc.id, soap(a.user_id), None, Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        s.finalise(&a, n.id, false, Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        let err = s.edit(&a, n.id, 2, soap(a.user_id), Utc::now()).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Domain(DomainError::ImmutableField)));
        let am = s.amend(&a, n.id, soap(a.user_id), Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(am.amends_note_id, Some(n.id));
        assert_eq!(am.status, medassist_domain::note::NoteStatus::Draft);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn cannot_draft_on_cancelled_encounter() {
        let s = svc();
        let a = actor("practitioner");
        let mut enc = NewEncounter { org_id: a.org_id, patient_id: Uuid::new_v4(), practitioner_id: a.user_id, chief_complaint: None }
            .into_encounter(Uuid::new_v4(), Utc::now());
        enc.transition(EncounterStatus::Cancelled, Utc::now()).unwrap();
        s.encounters.insert(&enc).await.unwrap();
        let err = s.create_draft(&a, enc.id, soap(a.user_id), None, Utc::now()).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Domain(DomainError::Validation { .. })));
    }
}

//! Patient use-cases: create with duplicate *flagging* (never auto-merge),
//! get/search/update, all org-scoped through the actor.

use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use uuid::Uuid;

use medassist_domain::patient::{NewPatient, Patient};
use medassist_domain::DomainError;

use crate::errors::{AppError, Actor};
use crate::ports::{AuditEvent, AuditOutcome, AuditSink, Page, PatientQuery, PatientRepository};

/// Score at/above which creation surfaces duplicate candidates for review.
pub const DUPLICATE_REVIEW_THRESHOLD: f64 = 0.6;

pub struct PatientService<R: PatientRepository, A: AuditSink> {
    pub repo: R,
    pub audit: A,
}

impl<R: PatientRepository, A: AuditSink> PatientService<R, A> {
    pub fn new(repo: R, audit: A) -> Self {
        Self { repo, audit }
    }

    /// Create a patient. If strong duplicate candidates exist, the creation is
    /// *blocked* with [`DomainError::DuplicateCandidate`] listing candidate
    /// ids — a human must decide (merge manually or proceed with distinct id).
    pub async fn create(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        input: NewPatient,
        allow_duplicates: bool,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Patient, AppError> {
        if input.org_id != actor.org_id && !actor.is_admin() {
            self.audit(actor, None, "patient.create", "denied").await;
            return Err(AppError::Forbidden("cannot create patients for another organisation"));
        }
        input.validate()?;

        if !allow_duplicates {
            let keys: Vec<String> = input.external_ids.iter().map(|e| e.normalised_key()).collect();
            let candidates = self
                .repo
                .duplicate_candidates(actor, format!("{} {}", input.given_name, input.family_name), input.birth_date, keys)
                .await?;
            let strong: Vec<Uuid> = candidates
                .into_iter()
                .filter(|(_, score)| *score >= DUPLICATE_REVIEW_THRESHOLD)
                .map(|(p, _)| p.id)
                .collect();
            if !strong.is_empty() {
                self.audit(actor, None, "patient.create.duplicate_blocked", "failure").await;
                return Err(DomainError::DuplicateCandidate(
                    strong.iter().map(|u| u.to_string()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(","),
                )
                .into());
            }
        }

        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let patient = input.into_patient(id, now);
        self.repo.insert(&patient).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(patient.id), "patient.create", "success").await;
        Ok(patient)
    }

    pub async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Patient, AppError> {
        match self.repo.get(actor, id).await? {
            Some(p) => Ok(p),
            None => {
                self.audit(actor, Some(id), "patient.read", "denied").await;
                Err(AppError::NotFound("patient"))
            }
        }
    }

    pub async fn search(&self, actor: &Actor, q: PatientQuery) -> Result<Page<Patient>, AppError> {
        self.repo.search(actor, &q).await
    }

    /// Update mutable demographics. Identity fields (id/org) are not editable.
    pub async fn update(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        id: Uuid,
        family_name: Option<String>,
        given_name: Option<String>,
        birth_date: Option<Option<chrono::NaiveDate>>,
        sex: Option<Option<medassist_domain::patient::Sex>>,
        contact: Option<Option<String>>,
        notes: Option<Option<String>>,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Patient, AppError> {
        let mut p = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        if let Some(v) = family_name {
            if v.trim().is_empty() {
                return Err(DomainError::validation("family_name", "must not be empty").into());
            }
            p.family_name = v.trim().to_owned();
        }
        if let Some(v) = given_name {
            p.given_name = v.trim().to_owned();
        }
        if let Some(v) = birth_date {
            if let Some(bd) = v {
                if bd > now.date_naive() {
                    return Err(DomainError::validation("birth_date", "cannot be in the future").into());
                }
            }
            p.birth_date = v;
        }
        if let Some(v) = sex {
            p.sex = v;
        }
        if let Some(v) = contact {
            p.contact = v.filter(|c| !c.trim().is_empty());
        }
        if let Some(v) = notes {
            p.notes = v.filter(|n| !n.trim().is_empty());
        }
        p.updated_at = now;
        self.repo.update(&p).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(p.id), "patient.update", "success").await;
        Ok(p)
    }

    /// Soft delete (tombstone). Hard deletion/retention is an ops policy.
    pub async fn soft_delete(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        if !actor.has_role("recorder") && !actor.is_admin() {
            self.audit(actor, Some(id), "patient.delete", "denied").await;
            return Err(AppError::Forbidden("requires recorder or admin role"));
        }
        let removed = self.repo.soft_delete(actor, id, now).await?;
        if !removed {
            return Err(AppError::NotFound("patient"));
        }
        self.audit(actor, Some(id), "patient.soft_delete", "success").await;
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn audit(&self, actor: &Actor, resource_id: Option<Uuid>, action: &str, outcome: &str) {
        self.audit
            .record(AuditEvent {
                time: Utc::now(),
                actor_user_id: Some(actor.user_id),
                org_id: Some(actor.org_id),
                action: action.to_owned(),
                resource_type: "patient".to_owned(),
                resource_id,
                outcome: match outcome {
                    "success" => AuditOutcome::Success,
                    "denied" => AuditOutcome::Denied,
                    _ => AuditOutcome::Failure,
                },
                metadata: serde_json::json!({}),
            })
            .await;
    }
}

//! Ports (traits) implemented by the infrastructure layer. The application
//! depends only on these abstractions — never on SQLx/Axum/provider SDKs.

use async_trait::async_trait;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use uuid::Uuid;

use medassist_domain::document::ClinicalDocument;
use medassist_domain::encounter::Encounter;
use medassist_domain::job::{AsyncJob, JobKind, JobState};

use medassist_domain::patient::Patient;
use medassist_domain::prov::Provenance;
use medassist_domain::transcription::{RecordingConsent, Transcription, TranscriptSegment};

use crate::errors::{AppError, Actor};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Page<T> {
    pub items: Vec<T>,
    pub total: i64,
    pub limit: i64,
    pub offset: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct PatientQuery {
    pub text: Option<String>,
    pub limit: i64,
    pub offset: i64,
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait PatientRepository: Send + Sync {
    async fn insert(&self, p: &Patient) -> Result<(), AppError>;
    async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<Patient>, AppError>;
    async fn search(&self, actor: &Actor, q: &PatientQuery) -> Result<Page<Patient>, AppError>;
    async fn update(&self, p: &Patient) -> Result<(), AppError>;
    async fn soft_delete(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<bool, AppError>;
    /// Candidates for duplicate review (advisory only — never auto-merged).
    async fn duplicate_candidates(&self, actor: &Actor, name: String, birth: Option<chrono::NaiveDate>, external_keys: Vec<String>) -> Result<Vec<(Patient, f64)>, AppError>;
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait EncounterRepository: Send + Sync {
    async fn insert(&self, e: &Encounter) -> Result<(), AppError>;
    async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<Encounter>, AppError>;
    async fn list_for_patient(&self, actor: &Actor, patient_id: Uuid, limit: i64, offset: i64) -> Result<Page<Encounter>, AppError>;
    async fn update(&self, e: &Encounter) -> Result<(), AppError>;
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait NoteRepository: Send + Sync {
    async fn insert(&self, n: &Note) -> Result<(), AppError>;
    async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<Note>, AppError>;
    async fn list_for_encounter(&self, actor: &Actor, encounter_id: Uuid) -> Result<Vec<Note>, AppError>;
    /// Persist an edit *and* its snapshot atomically; version guard enforced
    /// in SQL (`WHERE version = expected`) so races fail with a conflict.
    async fn update_with_version_guard(&self, n: &Note, expected_version: u32, edited_by: Uuid) -> Result<(), AppError>;
    async fn append_version_snapshot(&self, n: &Note, edited_by: Uuid) -> Result<(), AppError>;
}

    async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<ClinicalDocument>, AppError>;
    async fn set_state(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid, state: medassist_domain::document::ProcessingState, error: Option<String>) -> Result<(), AppError>;
    async fn list_for_patient(&self, actor: &Actor, patient_id: Uuid, limit: i64, offset: i64) -> Result<Page<ClinicalDocument>, AppError>;
}

/// Blob storage port. Implementations must keep originals immutable.
#[async_trait]
pub trait BlobStore: Send + Sync {
    async fn put(&self, org_id: Uuid, doc_id: Uuid, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<String, AppError>;
    async fn get(&self, key: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError>;
    async fn delete(&self, key: &str) -> Result<(), AppError>;
}

/// Blanket forwarding of the audit-sink port through shared references so
/// services can hold `Arc<dyn AuditSink>` without monomorphising per adapter.
#[async_trait]
impl<T: ?Sized + AuditSink + Sync> AuditSink for std::sync::Arc<T> {
    async fn record(&self, event: AuditEvent) {
        (**self).record(event).await
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl<T: ?Sized + BlobStore + Sync> BlobStore for std::sync::Arc<T> {
    async fn put(&self, org_id: Uuid, doc_id: Uuid, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<String, AppError> {
        (**self).put(org_id, doc_id, bytes).await
    }
    async fn get(&self, key: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
        (**self).get(key).await
    }
    async fn delete(&self, key: &str) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        (**self).delete(key).await
    }
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait ConsentRepository: Send + Sync {
    async fn insert(&self, c: &RecordingConsent) -> Result<(), AppError>;
    async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<RecordingConsent>, AppError>;
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait TranscriptionRepository: Send + Sync {
    async fn insert(&self, t: &Transcription) -> Result<(), AppError>;
    async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<Transcription>, AppError>;
    async fn complete(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid, segments: Vec<TranscriptSegment>, engine: &str, engine_version: Option<String>, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError>;
    async fn fail(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid, error: String, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError>;
    async fn mark_reviewed(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError>;
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait JobRepository: Send + Sync {
    async fn insert(&self, j: &AsyncJob) -> Result<(), AppError>;
    async fn claim_next(&self, kind: JobKind) -> Result<Option<AsyncJob>, AppError>;
    async fn transition(&self, id: Uuid, from: JobState, to: JobState, error: Option<String>, result_ref: Option<String>) -> Result<bool, AppError>;
    async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<AsyncJob>, AppError>;
}

/// Audit trail port. Implementations store metadata only — never full
/// clinical payloads (see docs/SECURITY).
#[async_trait]
pub trait AuditSink: Send + Sync {
    async fn record(&self, event: AuditEvent);
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct AuditEvent {
    pub time: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub actor_user_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub org_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub action: String,
    pub resource_type: String,
    pub resource_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub outcome: AuditOutcome,
    /// Non-clinical detail (ids, versions, flags). Never free-text PHI.
    pub metadata: serde_json::Value,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum AuditOutcome {
    Success,
    Failure,
    Denied,
}

//! Template listing use-case. Built-in SOAP template always exists; org
//! templates come from storage. Rendering/validation rules live here so the
//! API stays thin.

use medassist_domain::note::SoapContent;

use crate::errors::{AppError, Actor};
use crate::ports::{NoteTemplate, TemplateRepository};

pub const BUILTIN_SOAP_ID: &str = "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";

pub fn builtin_templates() -> Vec<NoteTemplate> {
    let uuid = |s: &str| uuid::Uuid::parse_str(s).expect("valid built-in uuid");
    vec![
        NoteTemplate {
            id: uuid(BUILTIN_SOAP_ID),
            org_id: None,
            name: "SOAP".into(),
            version: 1,
            sections: SoapSections::ALL.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect(),
            required_sections: vec!["subjective".into()],
        },
        NoteTemplate {
            id: uuid("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002"),
            org_id: None,
            name: "SOAP completo (todas as seções obrigatórias)".into(),
            version: 1,
            sections: SoapSections::ALL.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect(),
            required_sections: SoapSections::ALL.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect(),
        },
    ]
}

/// Canonical SOAP section names.
pub struct SoapSections;
impl SoapSections {
    pub const ALL: [&'static str; 4] = ["subjective", "objective", "assessment", "plan"];
}

pub struct TemplateService<R: TemplateRepository> {
    pub repo: R,
}

impl<R: TemplateRepository> TemplateService<R> {
    pub fn new(repo: R) -> Self {
        Self { repo }
    }

    pub async fn list(&self, actor: &Actor) -> Result<Vec<NoteTemplate>, AppError> {
        let mut all = builtin_templates();
        all.extend(self.repo.list(actor).await?);
        Ok(all)
    }

    /// Which required sections are still missing from a draft body — used by
    /// the editor to show gaps without inventing content.
    pub fn missing_required(template: &NoteTemplate, body: &medassist_domain::note::NoteBody) -> Vec<String> {
        let present = match body {
            medassist_domain::note::NoteBody::Soap(s) => present_soap_sections(s),
            medassist_domain::note::NoteBody::FreeText { .. } => vec!["text"],
        };
        template.required_sections.iter().filter(|r| !present.contains(&r.as_str())).cloned().collect()
    }
}

/// Names of non-empty SOAP sections.
pub fn present_soap_sections(s: &SoapContent) -> Vec<&'static str> {
    SoapSections::ALL
        .iter()
        .zip([&s.subjective, &s.objective, &s.assessment, &s.plan])
        .filter_map(|(n, slot)| slot.as_ref().filter(|x| !x.text.trim().is_empty()).map(|_| *n))
        .collect()
}

===== FILE 30/58: crates/medassist-application/src/transcription.rs (13528 bytes) =====
//! Transcription use-cases: consent-gated capture, engine-agnostic job
//! creation, review flow and conversion into a clinical draft.

use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use uuid::Uuid;

use medassist_domain::note::{NoteBody, SoapContent, SoapSection};
use medassist_domain::prov::{Provenance, ProvenanceKind, SourceRef};
use medassist_domain::transcription::{RecordingConsent, Transcription, TranscriptionState};
use medassist_domain::DomainError;

use crate::errors::{AppError, Actor};
use crate::ports::{AuditEvent, AuditOutcome, AuditSink, ConsentRepository, TranscriptionRepository};

/// Engine port. Implementations decide internally whether they provide
/// timestamps/confidence/diarisation — the contract never promises them.
#[async_trait::async_trait]
pub trait TranscriptionEngine: Send + Sync {
    fn id(&self) -> String;
    fn supports_timestamps(&self) -> bool;
    fn supports_confidence(&self) -> bool;
    fn supports_diarisation(&self) -> bool;
    /// Whether audio leaves the deployment boundary (remote service).
    fn is_external(&self) -> bool;
    async fn transcribe(&self, audio: &[u8], language: Option<&str>) -> Result<Vec<medassist_domain::transcription::TranscriptSegment>, AppError>;
}

pub struct TranscriptionService<T: TranscriptionRepository, C: ConsentRepository, A: AuditSink> {
    pub repo: T,
    pub consents: C,
    pub audit: A,
}

impl<T: TranscriptionRepository, C: ConsentRepository, A: AuditSink> TranscriptionService<T, C, A> {
    pub fn new(repo: T, consents: C, audit: A) -> Self {
        Self { repo, consents, audit }
    }

    pub async fn record_consent(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        patient_id: Uuid,
        granted_by: String,
        scope: String,
        retention_policy: String,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<RecordingConsent, AppError> {
        if granted_by.trim().is_empty() || scope.trim().is_empty() || retention_policy.trim().is_empty() {
            return Err(DomainError::validation("consent", "granted_by, scope and retention policy are required").into());
        }
        let c = RecordingConsent {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            org_id: actor.org_id,
            patient_id,
            granted_by: granted_by.trim().to_owned(),
            scope: scope.trim().to_owned(),
            retention_policy: retention_policy.trim().to_owned(),
            granted_at: now,
            revoked_at: None,
        };
        self.consents.insert(&c).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(c.id), "consent.grant", AuditOutcome::Success).await;
        Ok(c)
    }

    /// Mark a transcription as human-reviewed. Required before its text may
    /// become part of a clinical draft (see `to_draft_note_body`).
    pub async fn mark_reviewed(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        // Authorised read first (org-scoped), then persist the review flag.
        let _t = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        self.repo.mark_reviewed(actor, id, now).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(id), "transcription.review", AuditOutcome::Success).await;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Create a pending transcription. MUST fail without a valid consent —
    /// silent/background recording is impossible by construction here.
    pub async fn start(
        &self,
        actor: &Actor,
        patient_id: Uuid,
        encounter_id: Option<Uuid>,
        consent_id: Uuid,
        engine: &str,
        retention_policy: String,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Transcription, AppError> {
        let consent = self
            .consents
            .get(actor, consent_id)
            .await?
            .ok_or_else(|| AppError::Unprocessable("unknown consent".into()))?;
        if consent.patient_id != patient_id {
            self.audit(actor, Some(consent_id), "transcription.start.consent_mismatch", AuditOutcome::Denied).await;
            return Err(AppError::Forbidden("consent does not cover this patient"));
        }
        if !consent.is_valid_at(now) {
            self.audit(actor, Some(consent_id), "transcription.start.consent_invalid", AuditOutcome::Denied).await;
            return Err(AppError::Unprocessable("consent is revoked or not yet valid".into()));
        }
        let t = Transcription {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            org_id: actor.org_id,
            patient_id,
            encounter_id,
            state: TranscriptionState::Pending,
            engine: engine.to_owned(),
            engine_version: None,
            language: Some("pt-BR".into()),
            segments: vec![],
            reviewed: false,
            consent_id,
            audio_retention_policy: retention_policy,
            created_at: now,
            updated_at: now,
        };
        self.repo.insert(&t).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(t.id), "transcription.start", AuditOutcome::Success).await;
        Ok(t)
    }

    pub async fn cancel(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let t = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        if t.state == TranscriptionState::Completed {
            return Err(DomainError::IllegalTransition { from: "completed".into(), to: "cancelled".into() }.into());
        }
        self.repo.fail(actor, id, "cancelled_by_user".into(), now).await?;
        self.audit(actor, Some(id), "transcription.cancel", AuditOutcome::Success).await;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn get(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Transcription, AppError> {
        match self.repo.get(actor, id).await? {
            Some(t) => Ok(t),
            None => Err(AppError::NotFound("transcription")),
        }
    }

    /// Convert a *reviewed* transcription into a SOAP subjective draft with
    /// provenance pointing back at the transcription.
    pub async fn to_draft_note_body(&self, actor: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<NoteBody, AppError> {
        let t = self.get(actor, id).await?;
        if t.state != TranscriptionState::Completed {
            return Err(AppError::Unprocessable("transcription is not completed".into()));
        }
        if !t.reviewed {
            return Err(AppError::Unprocessable(
                "transcription must be reviewed/corrected before becoming a clinical draft".into(),
            ));
        }
        let text = t.full_text();
        if text.trim().is_empty() {
            return Err(AppError::Unprocessable("empty transcription".into()));
        }
        Ok(NoteBody::Soap(SoapContent {
            subjective: Some(SoapSection {
                text,
                provenance: Provenance::new(
                    ProvenanceKind::Transcription,
                    SourceRef::Transcription { id: t.id, start_ms: None, end_ms: None },
                ),
                reviewed: true, // human already reviewed the transcript itself
            }),
            ..Default::default()
        }))
    }

    async fn audit(&self, actor: &Actor, resource_id: Option<Uuid>, action: &str, outcome: AuditOutcome) {
        self.audit
            .record(AuditEvent {
                time: Utc::now(),
                actor_user_id: Some(actor.user_id),
                org_id: Some(actor.org_id),
                action: action.into(),
                resource_type: "transcription".into(),
                resource_id,
                outcome,
                metadata: serde_json::json!({}),
            })
            .await;
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::collections::HashMap;
    use std::sync::Mutex;

    use medassist_domain::transcription::TranscriptSegment;
    use crate::ports::Page;

    #[derive(Default)]
    struct MemTranscriptions(Mutex<HashMap<Uuid, Transcription>>);

    #[async_trait::async_trait]
    impl TranscriptionRepository for MemTranscriptions {
        async fn insert(&self, t: &Transcription) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            self.0.lock().unwrap().insert(t.id, t.clone());
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn get(&self, _a: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<Transcription>, AppError> {
            Ok(self.0.lock().unwrap().get(&id).cloned())
        }
        async fn complete(&self, _a: &Actor, id: Uuid, segments: Vec<TranscriptSegment>, engine: &str, _ev: Option<String>, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            let mut g = self.0.lock().unwrap();
            let t = g.get_mut(&id).ok_or(AppError::NotFound("t"))?;
            t.segments = segments;
            t.engine = engine.into();
            t.state = TranscriptionState::Completed;
            t.updated_at = now;
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn fail(&self, _a: &Actor, id: Uuid, _err: String, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            if let Some(t) = self.0.lock().unwrap().get_mut(&id) {
                t.state = TranscriptionState::Cancelled;
                t.updated_at = now;
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn mark_reviewed(&self, _a: &Actor, id: Uuid, _now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            if let Some(t) = self.0.lock().unwrap().get_mut(&id) {
                t.reviewed = true;
            }
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    #[derive(Default)]
    struct MemConsents(Mutex<HashMap<Uuid, RecordingConsent>>);

    #[async_trait::async_trait]
    impl ConsentRepository for MemConsents {
        async fn insert(&self, c: &RecordingConsent) -> Result<(), AppError> {
            self.0.lock().unwrap().insert(c.id, c.clone());
            Ok(())
        }
        async fn get(&self, _a: &Actor, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<RecordingConsent>, AppError> {
            Ok(self.0.lock().unwrap().get(&id).cloned())
        }
    }

    #[derive(Default)]
    struct MemAudit(Mutex<Vec<AuditEvent>>);
    #[async_trait::async_trait]
    impl AuditSink for MemAudit {
        async fn record(&self, ev: AuditEvent) {
            self.0.lock().unwrap().push(ev);
        }
    }

    fn svc() -> TranscriptionService<MemTranscriptions, MemConsents, MemAudit> {
        TranscriptionService::new(MemTranscriptions::default(), MemConsents::default(), MemAudit::default())
    }

    fn actor() -> Actor {
        Actor { user_id: Uuid::new_v4(), org_id: Uuid::new_v4(), roles: vec!["practitioner".into()] }
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn start_requires_valid_consent_for_same_patient() {
        let s = svc();
        let a = actor();
        let pid = Uuid::new_v4();
        let err = s.start(&a, pid, None, Uuid::new_v4(), "double", "keep_until_finalisation".into(), Utc::now()).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Unprocessable(_)));

        let c = s.record_consent(&a, pid, "paciente".into(), "áudio da consulta".into(), "delete_after_finalisation".into(), Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        let t = s.start(&a, pid, None, c.id, "double", "delete_after_finalisation".into(), Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(t.state, TranscriptionState::Pending);

        // other patient with same consent -> forbidden
        let other = Uuid::new_v4();
        let err = s.start(&a, other, None, c.id, "double", "x".into(), Utc::now()).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Forbidden(_)));

        // revoked consent -> refused
        let revoked = RecordingConsent { revoked_at: Some(Utc::now() - chrono::Duration::seconds(1)), ..c.clone() };
        s.consents.0.lock().unwrap().insert(revoked.id, revoked);
        let err = s.start(&a, pid, None, c.id, "double", "x".into(), Utc::now()).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Unprocessable(_)));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn unreviewed_transcription_cannot_become_draft() {
        let s = svc();
        let a = actor();
        let pid = Uuid::new_v4();
        let c = s.record_consent(&a, pid, "paciente".into(), "áudio".into(), "policy".into(), Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        let t = s.start(&a, pid, None, c.id, "double", "policy".into(), Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        // complete it via repo directly (worker would do this)
        s.repo.complete(&a, t.id, vec![TranscriptSegment { start_ms: 0, end_ms: 2000, text: "relato".into(), confidence: None, speaker: None }], "double", None, Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        let err = s.to_draft_note_body(&a, t.id).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Unprocessable(_)));
        s.repo.mark_reviewed(&a, t.id, Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        let body = s.to_draft_note_body(&a, t.id).await.unwrap();
        match body {
            NoteBody::Soap(x) => {
                let sec = x.subjective.unwrap();
                assert_eq!(sec.provenance.kind, ProvenanceKind::Transcription);
                assert!(sec.reviewed);
            }
            _ => panic!(),
        }
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn cancel_sets_state_and_audits() {
        let s = svc();
        let a = actor();
        let pid = Uuid::new_v4();
        let c = s.record_consent(&a, pid, "p".into(), "s".into(), "r".into(), Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        let t = s.start(&a, pid, None, c.id, "double", "r".into(), Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        s.cancel(&a, t.id, Utc::now()).await.unwrap();
        let got = s.get(&a, t.id).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(got.state, TranscriptionState::Cancelled);
        assert!(s.audit.0.lock().unwrap().iter().any(|e| e.action == "transcription.cancel"));
    }

    // silence unused import warning helper
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    fn _page_type_used(_: Page<u8>) {}
}

//! Allergy records with confirmation state and structured severity.

use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::error::DomainError;
use crate::prov::Provenance;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum AllergySeverity {
    Mild,
    Moderate,
    Severe,
    Unknown,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum AllergyConfirmation {
    /// Documented by a professional.
    Confirmed,
    /// Reported but not yet verified.
    Unverified,
    /// Previously recorded, now disputed/removed on review.
    EnteredInError,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Allergy {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub org_id: Uuid,
    pub patient_id: Uuid,
    /// Substance or class name, e.g. "Dipirona", "Penicilina".
    pub substance: String,
    pub reaction: Option<String>,
    pub severity: AllergySeverity,
    pub confirmation: AllergyConfirmation,
    pub provenance: Provenance,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

impl Allergy {
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), DomainError> {
        if self.substance.trim().is_empty() {
            return Err(DomainError::validation("substance", "allergy substance must not be empty"));
        }
        if self.substance.chars().count() > 200 {
            return Err(DomainError::validation("substance", "allergy substance too long"));
        }
        // A confirmed allergy must come from a confirmed source.
        if self.confirmation == AllergyConfirmation::Confirmed
            && !self.provenance.kind.is_confirmed_fact()
        {
            return Err(DomainError::ProvenanceRequired(self.provenance.kind.as_str().to_owned()));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::prov::{ProvenanceKind, SourceRef};

    fn a(conf: AllergyConfirmation, kind: ProvenanceKind) -> Allergy {
        Allergy {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            org_id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            patient_id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            substance: "Dipirona".into(),
            reaction: Some("urticária".into()),
            severity: AllergySeverity::Moderate,
            confirmation: conf,
            provenance: Provenance::new(kind, SourceRef::Practitioner { user_id: Uuid::new_v4() }),
            created_at: Utc::now(),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn unverified_ai_reported_allergy_is_allowed_but_flagged() {
        let x = a(AllergyConfirmation::Unverified, ProvenanceKind::Transcription);
        x.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn confirmed_allergy_needs_professional_provenance() {
        let x = a(AllergyConfirmation::Confirmed, ProvenanceKind::AiSuggestion);
        assert!(matches!(x.validate(), Err(DomainError::ProvenanceRequired(_))));
        let y = a(AllergyConfirmation::Confirmed, ProvenanceKind::ProfessionalEntry);
        y.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn empty_substance_rejected() {
        let mut x = a(AllergyConfirmation::Unverified, ProvenanceKind::PatientReport);
        x.substance = " ".into();
        assert!(x.validate().is_err());
    }
}
